function selectedTextOverlay(){
  return overlays.find(o=>o.id===selectedClip && o.type==='text') || null;
}
function renderTextPanel(){
  const host = document.getElementById('textPanelBody');
  if(!host) return;
  const clip = selectedTextOverlay();
  const family = clip?.fontFamily || pendingAdminFontFamily || 'Inter';
  const fontOptions = (group)=>group.map(f=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(f.value)}" ${family===f.value?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(f.label)}</option>`).join('');
  const adminFonts=getEditorAssets().filter(a=>editorAssetCategory(a)==='font').map(a=>({value:String(a.fontFamily||a.name||'').trim(),label:(a.name||a.fontFamily||'Custom font')+' — Admin font',asset:a})).filter(f=>f.value);
  const adminFontOptions=adminFonts.map(f=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(f.value)}" ${family===f.value?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(f.label)}</option>`).join('');
  if(family) ensureAdminFontLoaded(family);
  pendingAdminFontFamily='';
  host.innerHTML = `
    <div class="text-editor-controls">
      <div>
        <label class="text-field-label" for="textContentInput">Text / লেখা</label>
        <textarea id="textContentInput" maxlength="240" placeholder="এখানে বাংলা বা English লিখুন..." oninput="updateTextPreview()">${escapeHtml(clip?.textContent || '')}</textarea>
      </div>
      <div>
        <label class="text-field-label" for="textFontFamily">Font / ফন্ট</label>
        <select id="textFontFamily" onchange="updateTextPreview()">
          <optgroup label="English fonts">${fontOptions(ETHVELLE_TEXT_FONTS.filter(f=>f.script!=='bn'))}</optgroup>
          <optgroup label="বাংলা ফন্ট">${fontOptions(ETHVELLE_TEXT_FONTS.filter(f=>f.script==='bn'))}</optgroup>
          ${adminFontOptions?`<optgroup label="Admin fonts">${adminFontOptions}</optgroup>`:''}
        </select>
      </div>
      <div class="text-control-row">
        <div>
          <label class="text-field-label" for="textFontSize">Font size / সাইজ</label>
          <div class="text-range-line"><input id="textFontSize" type="range" min="16" max="96" step="1" value="${Math.max(16,Math.min(96,Number(clip?.fontSize)||36))}" oninput="updateTextPreview()"><output id="textFontSizeValue">${Math.max(16,Math.min(96,Number(clip?.fontSize)||36))}px</output></div>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label class="text-field-label" for="textFontColor">Color / রং</label>
          <input id="textFontColor" type="color" value="${/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(clip?.textColor||'')?clip.textColor:'#ffffff'}" oninput="updateTextPreview()">
        </div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <label class="text-field-label">Preview / প্রিভিউ</label>
        <div id="textLivePreview" class="text-live-preview">আপনার লেখা এখানে</div>
      </div>
      <button type="button" class="text-save-btn" onclick="saveTextOverlay()">${clip?'Update selected text':'Add text to timeline'}</button>
      <p class="chroma-note">বাংলা ও English—দুই ধরনের লেখা ব্যবহার করতে পারবেন। ফন্ট ঠিকভাবে লোড হওয়ার জন্য ইন্টারনেট সংযোগ প্রয়োজন হতে পারে।</p>
    </div>`;
  updateTextPreview();
}
function updateTextPreview(){
  const textEl=document.getElementById('textContentInput');
  const fontEl=document.getElementById('textFontFamily');
  const sizeEl=document.getElementById('textFontSize');
  const colorEl=document.getElementById('textFontColor');
  const preview=document.getElementById('textLivePreview');
  if(!preview) return;
  const text=textEl?.value || 'আপনার লেখা এখানে';
  const font=fontEl?.value || 'Hind Siliguri';
  ensureAdminFontLoaded(font);
  const size=Math.max(16,Math.min(96,Number(sizeEl?.value)||36));
  preview.textContent=text;
  preview.style.fontFamily='"'+font+'", sans-serif';
  preview.style.fontSize=Math.min(34,size)+'px';
  preview.style.color=colorEl?.value || '#ffffff';
  const out=document.getElementById('textFontSizeValue');
  if(out) out.textContent=size+'px';
}
function saveTextOverlay(){
  const text=String(document.getElementById('textContentInput')?.value||'').trim();
  if(!text){showToast('Write some text first', {icon:toastIcons.info,duration:1500});return;}
  const family=document.getElementById('textFontFamily')?.value || 'Hind Siliguri';
  ensureAdminFontLoaded(family);
  const size=Math.max(16,Math.min(96,Number(document.getElementById('textFontSize')?.value)||36));
  const color=document.getElementById('textFontColor')?.value || '#ffffff';
  let clip=selectedTextOverlay();
  if(clip){
    clip.textContent=text; clip.name=text.replace(/\s+/g,' ').slice(0,22); clip.fontFamily=family; clip.fontSize=size; clip.textColor=color; clip.fontWeight=700;
  }else{
    const len=5;
    const startT=(typeof window.ethvellePlanTextInsertStart==='function'?window.ethvellePlanTextInsertStart(len):Math.max(0,Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0));
    clip={id:'ov'+Date.now()+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,5),name:text.replace(/\s+/g,' ').slice(0,22)||'Text',color:'#454B73',type:'text',textContent:text,fontFamily:family,fontSize:size,textColor:color,fontWeight:700,sourceDuration:len,trimStart:startT,trimEnd:startT+len,thumbs:[],peaks:[],speed:1,transform:{scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0}};
    overlays.push(clip);
    timelineDuration=Math.max(timelineDuration,clip.trimEnd);
  }
  renderOverlayLayers(); syncTimelineInnerWidth(); renderRuler(); selectClipForChrome(clip.id); renderFrame(); closePanel(); persistProjectState();
  showToast(clip.type==='text'?'Text saved':'Text added', {icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1300});
}

// ---------------- Chroma key / color background removal ----------------
function getSelectedVideoOverlay(){
  if(!selectedClip) return null;
  const clip=overlays.find(o=>o.id===selectedClip);
  return clip && clip.type==='video' ? clip : null;
}
function renderChromaKeyPanel(){
  const host=document.getElementById('chromaPanelBody');
  if(!host)return;
  const clip=overlays.find(o=>String(o.id)===String(selectedClip)&&o.type==='video');
  if(!clip){host.innerHTML='<div class="chroma-controls"></div>';return;}
  if(!clip.chromaKey)clip.chromaKey={enabled:false,color:'#00ff00',tolerance:75,softness:24};
  const cfg=clip.chromaKey;
  host.innerHTML=`<div class="chroma-controls evlt-compact-controls">
    <label class="chroma-enable-row"><span>Chroma Key</span><input type="checkbox" ${cfg.enabled?'checked':''} onchange="setChromaEnabled(this.checked)"></label>
    <div class="chroma-color-row"><label for="chromaKeyColor">Key color</label><input id="chromaKeyColor" type="color" value="${/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(cfg.color||'')?cfg.color:'#00ff00'}" oninput="updateChromaSetting('color',this.value)"></div>
    <div class="chroma-range"><div class="chroma-range-top"><label for="chromaTolerance">Tolerance</label><output id="chromaToleranceValue">${Number(cfg.tolerance)||75}</output></div><input id="chromaTolerance" type="range" min="10" max="180" step="1" value="${Math.max(10,Math.min(180,Number(cfg.tolerance)||75))}" oninput="updateChromaSetting('tolerance',this.value)"></div>
    <div class="chroma-range"><div class="chroma-range-top"><label for="chromaSoftness">Edge softness</label><output id="chromaSoftnessValue">${Number.isFinite(Number(cfg.softness))?Number(cfg.softness):24}</output></div><input id="chromaSoftness" type="range" min="0" max="100" step="1" value="${Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number.isFinite(Number(cfg.softness))?Number(cfg.softness):24))}" oninput="updateChromaSetting('softness',this.value)"></div>
    <button type="button" class="chroma-reset-btn" onclick="resetChromaSettings()">Reset</button>
  </div>`;
}
function setChromaEnabled(enabled){
  const clip=getSelectedVideoOverlay(); if(!clip) return;
  if(!clip.chromaKey) clip.chromaKey={color:'#00ff00',tolerance:75,softness:24};
  clip.chromaKey.enabled=!!enabled;
  renderFrame(); persistProjectState();
}
function updateChromaSetting(key,value){
  const clip=getSelectedVideoOverlay(); if(!clip) return;
  if(!clip.chromaKey) clip.chromaKey={enabled:false,color:'#00ff00',tolerance:75,softness:24};
  if(key==='color') clip.chromaKey.color=String(value);
  else if(key==='tolerance') clip.chromaKey.tolerance=Math.max(10,Math.min(180,Number(value)||75));
  else if(key==='softness') clip.chromaKey.softness=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(value)||0));
  const out=document.getElementById(key==='tolerance'?'chromaToleranceValue':key==='softness'?'chromaSoftnessValue':null);
  if(out) out.textContent=String(clip.chromaKey[key]);
  renderFrame(); persistProjectState();
}
function resetChromaSettings(){
  const clip=getSelectedVideoOverlay(); if(!clip) return;
  clip.chromaKey={enabled:false,color:'#00ff00',tolerance:75,softness:24};
  renderChromaKeyPanel(); renderFrame(); persistProjectState();
}
function hexToRgb(hex){
  let h=String(hex||'#00ff00').replace('#','').trim();
  if(h.length===3) h=h.split('').map(ch=>ch+ch).join('');
  if(!/^[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(h)) h='00ff00';
  return {r:parseInt(h.slice(0,2),16),g:parseInt(h.slice(2,4),16),b:parseInt(h.slice(4,6),16)};
}
function paintChromaKeyFrame(source,canvas,cfg,maxSide){
  const sw=source?.videoWidth||source?.naturalWidth||source?.width||0;
  const sh=source?.videoHeight||source?.naturalHeight||source?.height||0;
  if(!sw||!sh) return false;
  const ratio=Math.min(1,(Number(maxSide)>0?Number(maxSide):Math.max(sw,sh))/Math.max(sw,sh));
  const w=Math.max(1,Math.round(sw*ratio)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(sh*ratio));
  if(canvas.width!==w) canvas.width=w; if(canvas.height!==h) canvas.height=h;
  const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
  if(!ctx) return false;
  try{
    ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h); ctx.drawImage(source,0,0,w,h);
    const frame=ctx.getImageData(0,0,w,h),d=frame.data,key=hexToRgb(cfg?.color);
    const tolerance=Math.max(0,Number(cfg?.tolerance)||75),softness=Math.max(0,Number(cfg?.softness)||0),edge=Math.max(1,softness);
    for(let i=0;i<d.length;i+=4){
      const dr=d[i]-key.r,dg=d[i+1]-key.g,db=d[i+2]-key.b;
      const distance=Math.sqrt(dr*dr+dg*dg+db*db);
      if(distance<=tolerance) d[i+3]=0;
      else if(softness>0 && distance<tolerance+softness) d[i+3]=Math.round(d[i+3]*Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(distance-tolerance)/edge)));
    }
    ctx.putImageData(frame,0,0); return true;
  }catch(err){
    console.warn('Ethvelle: chroma-key pixel processing failed (the video may be cross-origin)',err);
    return false;
  }
}

function openPanel(key){
  // BUGFIX (Animate panel could be opened with zero characters on stage): the Animate panel
  // lets you pick/replace an animation preset for "the active character" (see renderAnimList/
  // appendAnimationClip), which is meaningless with no character to apply it to —
  // activeCharacter() is null and bones/baseAngles are never set up yet. Previously the panel
  // still opened and let you pick a preset, which either silently did nothing useful or could
  // leave inconsistent state. Now it's blocked at the door with a clear nudge toward the real
  // first step — the same "Add character" affordance already on the timeline/Bones panel —
  // instead of opening into a panel that has nothing to actually animate yet.
  if(key === 'anim' && !characters.length){
    showToast('Add a character first', {icon: toastIcons.info, duration:1600});
    return;
  }
  document.querySelectorAll('.tool-item').forEach(t=>t.classList.remove('active'));
  const btn = document.querySelector(`.tool-item[data-tool="${key}"]`);
  if(btn) btn.classList.add('active');

  const content = panelContent[key];
  // Panel title reflects what Transform will actually edit (see transformTarget()) — so it's
  // clear at a glance whether Size/Position/Rotation below are about to move the Background,
  // a Clip, or the stickman, instead of the title always just saying "Transform" regardless.
  if(key === 'transform'){
    const { kind } = transformTarget();
    document.getElementById('panelTitle').textContent =
      kind==='background' ? 'Transform — Background' :
      kind==='overlay' ? 'Transform Clip' : 'Transform';
  } else {
    document.getElementById('panelTitle').textContent = content.title;
  }
  document.getElementById('panelBody').innerHTML = content.body;
  document.getElementById('panelBackdrop').classList.add('active');
  document.getElementById('toolPanel').classList.add('open');

  if(key === 'transform') syncTransformSliders();
  if(key === 'anim') renderAnimList();
  if(key === 'overlay') renderOverlayListPanel();
  if(key === 'assets') renderAssetsPanel();
  if(key === 'trim') renderTrimPanel();
  if(key === 'speed') renderSpeedPanel();
  if(key === 'volume') renderVolumePanel();
  if(key === 'text') renderTextPanel();
  if(key === 'chroma') renderChromaKeyPanel();
}
function closePanel(){
  document.getElementById('panelBackdrop').classList.remove('active');
  document.getElementById('toolPanel').classList.remove('open');
  document.querySelectorAll('.tool-item').forEach(t=>t.classList.remove('active'));
  // Closing the panel mid-preview (see previewAnim) should never leave the rig stuck on
  // some in-between preset frame — cancel the loop and restore the pose it started from.
  if(typeof previewRAF !== 'undefined' && previewRAF){
    cancelAnimationPreview(true);
    renderAnimList();
  }
}

function renderAnimList(){
  const container = document.getElementById('animListBody');
  if(!container) return;
  const selectedAnimKey = (()=>{
    if(selectedClip === 'main' && activeClip) return activeClip.animKey;
    if(typeof selectedClip === 'string' && selectedClip.startsWith('seg')){ const idx=parseInt(selectedClip.slice(3),10); return splitStickmanSegments[idx]?.animKey || null; }
    if(selectedClip == null) return activeClip?.animKey || null;
    return null;
  })();
  container.innerHTML = Object.keys(animPresets).map(key=>{
    const p=animPresets[key]; const applied=key===selectedAnimKey; const previewing=key===previewAnimKey;
    const required=getAnimationRequiredPlan(key); const locked=isAnimationLocked(key);
    const action=locked && !applied
      ? `<button type="button" class="anim-lock-btn" onclick="event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();openAnimationUpgrade('${escapeHtml(required.id)}')">🔒 ${escapeHtml(required.name||'Paid')}</button>`
      : `<button type="button" class="anim-toggle ${applied?'on':''}" aria-pressed="${applied?'true':'false'}" title="${applied?'Turn off':'Add animation'}" onclick="event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();toggleApplyAnim('${escapeHtml(key)}')"></button>`;
    return `<div class="anim-item ${previewing?'selected':''}" role="button" tabindex="0" onclick="previewAnim('${escapeHtml(key)}')" onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'||event.key===' '){event.preventDefault();previewAnim('${escapeHtml(key)}')}">
      <div class="anim-play-mini"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M8 5v14l11-7z"/></svg></div>
      <div class="anim-item-name">${escapeHtml(p.name||key)}</div><div class="anim-item-dur">${Number(p.duration||1).toFixed(1)}s</div>${action}
    </div>`;
  }).join('');
}

// Tapping the ROW ITSELF (not the toggle) now only PREVIEWS a preset — plays it briefly on
// the canvas so you can see the motion before committing to it — without touching the
// selected clip's saved animKey at all. This replaces the old behavior where a single tap
// both selected AND immediately applied the preset to whatever clip was selected.
let previewAnimKey = null;
let previewRAF = null;
let previewSnapshot = null;
let previewBonesRef = null;
let previewCharacterId = null;
let previewExprSnapshot = null;
function cancelAnimationPreview(redraw){
  if(previewRAF){ cancelAnimationFrame(previewRAF); previewRAF = null; }
  if(previewSnapshot && previewBonesRef) restoreBonesSnapshot(previewSnapshot, previewBonesRef);
  if(previewCharacterId && previewExprSnapshot){
    const targetChar=characters.find(c=>c&&c.id===previewCharacterId);
    if(targetChar)targetChar.expr=previewExprSnapshot.had?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(previewExprSnapshot.value)):null;
  }
  previewExprSnapshot = null; previewSnapshot = null; previewBonesRef = null; previewCharacterId = null; previewAnimKey = null;
  if(redraw !== false) drawStick();
}
// v344: a beast-man animation fits beast-man models only; plain humanoid animations still fit beast men too (same rig).
function animPresetFitsCharacter(preset,character){
  const pt=(preset&&preset.modelType)||'humanoid';
  const ct=String(character&&(character.modelType||(character.sheet&&character.sheet.modelType))||'humanoid');
  if(pt==='beastman')return ct==='humanoid'&&!!(character&&((character.sheet&&character.sheet.beast)||character.beast));
  if(pt==='mythic')return isMythicModelType(ct);
  if(pt==='humanoid'&&isWingedHumanoidType(ct))return true;
  return pt===ct;
}
function previewAnim(key){
  const character = activeCharacter();
  const preset = animPresets[key];
  if(!character || !bones || !preset) return;
  const activeType=String(character.modelType||(character.sheet&&character.sheet.modelType)||'humanoid');
  if(!animPresetFitsCharacter(preset,character)){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Select a matching character type for this animation.',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:1800});return;}
  pausePlayback();
  // A preview is temporary only. Keep its exact target rig so a character switch cannot
  // restore the preview pose into a different character by accident.
  cancelAnimationPreview(false);
  previewBonesRef = character.bones;
  previewCharacterId = character.id;
  previewSnapshot = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(previewBonesRef));
  previewExprSnapshot = {had:!!character.expr,value:character.expr?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(character.expr)):null};
  previewAnimKey = key;
  renderAnimList();
  const LOOPS = 2;
  const startTime = performance.now();
  const totalMs = preset.duration * 1000 * LOOPS;
  const step = (now)=>{
    // If the preview belongs to a character which is no longer the active canvas layer,
    // stop cleanly; never keep animating an invisible/stale rig.
    if(!previewBonesRef || !characters.some(c=>c.id===previewCharacterId) || !activeCharacter() || activeCharacter().id!==previewCharacterId){
      cancelAnimationPreview(false); drawStick(); renderAnimList(); return;
    }
    const elapsed = now - startTime;
    if(elapsed >= totalMs){
      cancelAnimationPreview(false);
      drawStick();
      renderAnimList();
      return;
    }
    const tFrac = (elapsed % (preset.duration*1000)) / (preset.duration*1000);
    applyPoseToBones(evalPreset(preset, tFrac), previewBonesRef, character.baseAngles, character.hipRestY);
    if(preset.faceActions){
      if(!character.expr)character.expr=typeof sheetExprFresh==='function'?sheetExprFresh():{};
      const fa=preset.faceActions,period=Math.max(.45,Math.min(6,Number(fa.blinkRate)||3.15)),phase=(elapsed/1000)%period,blink=fa.blink==='auto'&&(phase<.13||phase>period-.13);
      const talkPhase=(elapsed/1000)%0.82;
      const talkOpen=fa.mouth==='talk'&&(talkPhase<.22||(talkPhase>.38&&talkPhase<.58));
      const p=character.sheet&&character.sheet.parts||{},mt=String(character.modelType||(character.sheet&&character.sheet.modelType)||'');
      const animalFaceKit=mt==='animal'&&!!(p.mouth_inner&&p.head_laugh);
      const humanoidFaceKit=mt==='humanoid'&&!!(p.mouth_inner&&p.head_laugh&&p.eye_L&&p.eye_R);
      const birdFaceKit=mt==='bird'&&!!(p.bird_eye&&p.bird_brow&&p.beak_open&&p.beak_closed);
      character.expr.emo=fa.mouth==='talk'&&(animalFaceKit||humanoidFaceKit||birdFaceKit)
        ? (talkOpen?(fa.expression&&fa.expression!=='none'?fa.expression:'laugh'):null)
        : (fa.expression&&fa.expression!=='none'?fa.expression:null);
      character.expr.eyeState=['open','closed'].includes(fa.eyes)?fa.eyes:'auto';
      character.expr.blink=character.expr.eyeState==='closed'?false:!!blink;
      character.expr.ambientBlink=fa.blink==='ambient'&&phase<.13;
      character.expr.disableAmbientBlink=fa.blink==='off'||character.expr.eyeState!=='auto';
      character.expr.flying=birdFaceKit&&fa.wings==='fly';
      if(fa.mouth==='talk')character.expr.mouth=talkOpen?'open':'closed';
      else if(fa.mouth==='closed'||fa.mouth==='hold')character.expr.mouth=fa.mouth;
      else character.expr.mouth=birdFaceKit&&['angry','shock','laugh'].includes(fa.expression)?'open':'closed';
      character.expr.faceKitHideMouth=(animalFaceKit||humanoidFaceKit)&&(fa.mouth==='closed'||(fa.mouth==='talk'&&!talkOpen));
    }
    drawStick();
    previewRAF = requestAnimationFrame(step);
  };
  previewRAF = requestAnimationFrame(step);
}

// The toggle switch is the ONLY thing that actually commits a preset to the selected clip
// now (see the .anim-toggle CSS note). Tapping an already-on toggle turns the animation back
// off (the clip reverts to no preset / the character's rest pose) rather than being a one-way
// switch, since a real on/off control should go both ways.
function toggleApplyAnim(key){
  const preset = animPresets[key];
  const character=activeCharacter();
  if(!preset || !character) return;
  const activeType=String(character.modelType||(character.sheet&&character.sheet.modelType)||'humanoid');
  if(!animPresetFitsCharacter(preset,character)){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Select a matching character type for this animation.',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:1800});return;}
  cancelAnimationPreview(false);
  pausePlayback();

  // Only an explicitly selected main/split animation clip is a toggle target. If the user
  // has selected a background, overlay, or character row, tapping Add must create a new
  // animation clip instead of silently toggling currentAnimKey without changing the clip.
  let target = null;
  if(selectedClip === 'main' && activeClip){
    target = {ref:'main', clip:activeClip};
  } else if(typeof selectedClip === 'string' && selectedClip.startsWith('seg')){
    const idx = parseInt(selectedClip.slice(3),10);
    if(Number.isInteger(idx) && splitStickmanSegments[idx]) target = {ref:'seg'+idx, clip:splitStickmanSegments[idx]};
  } else if(selectedClip == null && activeClip){
    // No explicit selection means the main animation clip is the default target. This avoids
    // an apparent flaky toggle where the list UI showed the main animation as selected but
    // the click instead created a separate clip because selectedClip was null.
    target = {ref:'main', clip:activeClip};
  }
  const currentlyApplied = !!(target && target.clip.animKey === key);
  if(!currentlyApplied && isAnimationLocked(key)){
    openAnimationUpgrade(getAnimationRequiredPlan(key)?.id);
    drawStick();
    renderAnimList();
    return;
  }

  if(currentlyApplied){
    if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(target.clip,'__evFaceActionSnapshot')){
      const before=target.clip.__evFaceActionSnapshot;
      if(before===null)delete target.clip.faceActions;else target.clip.faceActions=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(before));
      delete target.clip.__evFaceActionSnapshot;
    }
    target.clip.animKey = null;
    if(target.ref === 'main') currentAnimKey = null;
    renderTimelineClip();
    renderAnimList();
    // renderFrame restores the authored rest pose when a clip has no active preset, so
    // repeatedly toggling an animation on/off cannot leave the character stuck mid-motion.
    renderFrame();
    showToast(preset.name + ' turned off', {duration:1200});
    persistProjectState();
    return;
  }

  if(target){
    target.clip.animKey = key;
    if(target.ref === 'main') currentAnimKey = key;
    renderTimelineClip();
    renderAnimList();
    seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds(target.clip.trimStart);
  } else if(activeClip){
    appendAnimationClip(key);
    renderAnimList();
  } else {
    setActiveAnimation(key);
    renderAnimList();
  }
  const committedFaceClip=target?target.clip:activeClip;
  if(preset.faceActions&&committedFaceClip){
    if(!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(committedFaceClip,'__evFaceActionSnapshot'))committedFaceClip.__evFaceActionSnapshot=committedFaceClip.faceActions?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(committedFaceClip.faceActions)):null;
    committedFaceClip.faceActions={expression:'none',mouth:'preset',blink:'auto',eyes:'auto',leftHand:'none',rightHand:'none',...preset.faceActions};
  }
  showToast(preset.name + (target ? ' applied' : ' added'), {icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1400});
  // Start at the chosen clip's first frame to make the newly committed animation visible
  // immediately. The row itself only previews; this real button is the commit action.
  renderFrame();
  startPlayback();
  persistProjectState();
}
// FEATURE (multiple sequential backgrounds): picking a solid-color swatch now APPENDS a new
// background clip (matching the image-upload path in addBackgroundClip) instead of
// overwriting the single project-wide background in place.
function pickBg(color, el){
  document.querySelectorAll('.bg-swatch').forEach(s=>s.classList.remove('selected'));
  el.classList.add('selected');
  addBackgroundClip(color);
  // UPDATED: adding a background used to leave the Background panel open when the background
  // came from a colour swatch (only the image-upload path called closePanel()), so the user had
  // to close it by hand every time. One tap on a swatch adds the 5s background AND closes the
  // panel now, matching the upload path and how CapCut dismisses its picker after a choice.
  closePanel();
}
function resetPose(){
  pausePlayback();
  currentAnimKey = null;
  activeClip = null;
  splitStickmanSegments = [];
  syncClipRefsToCharacter();
  timelineDuration = 2.0;
  deselectAllClips();
  initStickman();
  renderAnimList();
  renderTimelineClip();
  frame = 0;
  currentAbsoluteTimeSec = 0;
  updateTimeReadout();
  movePlayhead();
  showToast('Pose reset', {icon: toastIcons.check, duration:1200});
  persistProjectState();
}

let uploadTarget=null;
let uploadAppendGroup=null; // when set ('clips'|'audio'), the next uploaded clip appends after that group's last clip instead of dropping at the playhead (see clipAddMediaTool)
let replaceTargetId=null; // overlay clip id currently being replaced — see replaceSelectedClip()/the 'replace-overlay' upload branch
function triggerUpload(target, appendAfterGroup){
  uploadTarget = target;
  uploadAppendGroup = appendAfterGroup || null;
  const input = document.getElementById('fileInput');
  // Match CapCut: each entry point only accepts the media type it deals in.
  if(target==='overlay' || target==='media'){ input.accept = 'image/*,video/*'; }
  else if(target==='audio'){ input.accept = 'audio/*'; }
  else if(target==='background' || target==='replace-background'){ input.accept = 'image/*'; }
  // Replace can swap an existing clip for any media type (image/video/audio) — the actual
  // clip TYPE is decided by whichever file is picked (see the 'replace-overlay' branch),
  // not fixed by whatever the clip started out as.
  else if(target==='replace-overlay'){ input.accept = 'image/*,video/*,audio/*'; }
  else { input.accept = 'image/*'; }
  input.click();
}

// Dispatches the contextual toolbar's "Replace" button (see bottomToolsClip markup) based on
// what's currently selected — this replaces the old numeric Trim panel entirely, per request.
//  - Background selected: reopens the same image picker "Add background" already used —
//    picking a new image just overwrites currentBg in place, same as setting it the first time.
//  - A media/overlay clip selected: opens the picker and swaps that exact clip's own
//    picture/video/audio content in place (see the 'replace-overlay' upload branch), keeping
//    its position and trim untouched.
//  - The stickman animation clip (main/segN) selected: "replacing" it means swapping its
//    animation preset — opens the Animate panel, where the toggle already handles applying a
//    different preset onto the currently-selected clip in place.
function replaceSelectedClip(){
  if(!selectedClip) return;
  const bgClip = backgroundClipForSelection();
  if(bgClip){
    replaceTargetId = bgClip.id;
    triggerUpload('replace-background');
    return;
  }
  const isStickman = selectedClip==='main' || String(selectedClip).startsWith('seg');
  if(isStickman){
    openPanel('anim');
    return;
  }
  const ov = overlays.find(o=>o.id===selectedClip);
  if(ov){
    replaceTargetId = ov.id;
    triggerUpload('replace-overlay');
  }
}

/* ---------------- Real media generation: video thumbnails + audio waveforms ----------------
   CapCut shows the actual content of a clip in its track (filmstrip of real frames for
   video, a rendered waveform for audio) rather than a flat color block. We generate both
   client-side from the uploaded file so what's shown is genuine, not a placeholder. */

// Captures `count` evenly-spaced frames from a <video> as JPEG data URLs using an offscreen
// canvas. Resolves once all frames are captured (or on failure, resolves with []).
function generateVideoThumbnails(url, count){
  // Kept as a compatibility name for older upload handlers. Video frames are no longer
  // batch-extracted here; the timeline's single lazy decoder owns all video thumbnails.
  return new Promise(resolve=>{
    const video=document.createElement('video');
    let settled=false,timer=0;
    video.muted=true;video.playsInline=true;video.preload='metadata';
    video.setAttribute('playsinline','');
    if(/^https?:/i.test(String(url||'')))video.crossOrigin='anonymous';
    const finish=()=>{
      if(settled)return;settled=true;clearTimeout(timer);
      const result={frames:[],duration:Number(video.duration)||1,width:Number(video.videoWidth)||0,height:Number(video.videoHeight)||0};
      try{video.pause();video.removeAttribute('src');video.load();}catch(_e){}
      resolve(result);
    };
    video.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',finish,{once:true});
    video.addEventListener('error',finish,{once:true});
    timer=setTimeout(finish,15000);
    try{video.src=String(url||'');video.load();}catch(_e){finish();}
  });
}

// Decodes an audio file with Web Audio API and returns { peaks: Float32Array-like downsampled
// min/max pairs, duration } so we can draw a real waveform without keeping the whole PCM buffer.
function generateAudioWaveform(url){
  return fetch(url)
    .then(r=>r.arrayBuffer())
    .then(buf=>{
      const AC = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
      const actx = new AC();
      return actx.decodeAudioData(buf).then(audioBuffer=>{
        const raw = audioBuffer.getChannelData(0);
        const samples = 200; // resolution of the drawn waveform, independent of clip length
        const blockSize = Math.max(1, Math.floor(raw.length/samples));
        const peaks = [];
        for(let i=0;i<samples;i++){
          const start = i*blockSize;
          let min=1, max=-1;
          for(let j=0;j<blockSize && (start+j)<raw.length;j++){
            const v = raw[start+j];
            if(v<min) min=v;
            if(v>max) max=v;
          }
          peaks.push([min,max]);
        }
        const duration = audioBuffer.duration;
        actx.close();
        return { peaks, duration };
      });
    })
    .catch(()=>({ peaks: [], duration: 1 }));
}

document.getElementById('fileInput').addEventListener('change', function(e){
  const file = e.target.files[0];
  if(!file) return;
  const isVideo = file.type.startsWith('video/');
  const isAudio = file.type.startsWith('audio/');

  // Standard Android/WebView picker path: persist overlay media as an IndexedDB Blob with a
  // stable media key. The old Base64 path could push localStorage past its safety threshold;
  // the project list then became an index-only record before its delayed IndexedDB snapshot
  // had committed, so reopening could show missing overlay media. This branch saves the actual
  // source first, references it by key, and awaits the complete project snapshot after adding.
  const pickedTarget = String(uploadTarget||'');
  const pickedName = String(file.name||'');
  const pickedIsVideo = isVideo || /\.(mp4|m4v|f4v|mov|webm|mkv|avi|3gp|3gpp|3g2|mpeg|mpg|mpe|m2v|ogv|ts|mts|m2ts|wmv|flv)$/i.test(pickedName);
  const pickedIsAudio = isAudio || /\.(mp3|m4a|aac|wav|ogg|opus|flac|aiff?)$/i.test(pickedName);
  const pickedIsImage = /^image\//i.test(file.type||'') || /\.(png|apng|jpe?g|jfif|webp|gif|avif|bmp|dib|heic|heif|tif|tiff|svg|ico|jxl)$/i.test(pickedName);
  if(['overlay','media','audio','replace-overlay'].includes(pickedTarget) &&
     typeof window.__ethvelleStoreMediaFile==='function' && (pickedIsVideo||pickedIsAudio||pickedIsImage)){
    const pickedFile = file;
    const pickedAppendGroup = uploadAppendGroup || null;
    const pickedReplaceId = replaceTargetId;
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopImmediatePropagation(); // do not let the later legacy video listener import this twice
    closePanel();
    uploadAppendGroup = null;
    replaceTargetId = null;
    e.target.value='';
    (async()=>{
      let mediaKey=null, mediaUrl=null, sessionOnly=false;
      try{
        try{mediaKey = await window.__ethvelleStoreMediaFile(pickedFile);}catch(storageError){
          console.warn('Ethvelle durable media storage unavailable; using a safe import fallback:',storageError);
          if(pickedFile.size<=3*1024*1024){
            mediaUrl=await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const reader=new FileReader();reader.onload=()=>resolve(String(reader.result||''));reader.onerror=()=>reject(reader.error||new Error('Could not read the selected media.'));reader.readAsDataURL(pickedFile);});
          }else{mediaUrl=URL.createObjectURL(pickedFile);sessionOnly=true;}
        }
        if(!mediaUrl)mediaUrl = URL.createObjectURL(pickedFile);
        const type = pickedTarget==='audio' ? 'audio' : pickedIsVideo ? 'video' : pickedIsAudio ? 'audio' : 'image';
        let duration = type==='video' ? 12 : 3;
        let thumbs = [], peaks = [], sourceWidth=0, sourceHeight=0;
        if(type==='video'){
          showToast('Preparing video clip…',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:1200});
          const result = await generateVideoThumbnails(mediaUrl,24,0,null); // metadata only; frame work is lazy
          thumbs = [];
          duration = Math.max(MIN_CLIP_SEC,Number(result&&result.duration)||12);
          sourceWidth=Number(result&&result.width)||0;sourceHeight=Number(result&&result.height)||0;
        }else if(type==='audio'){
          showToast('Preparing audio clip…',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:1200});
          const result = await generateAudioWaveform(mediaUrl);
          peaks = Array.isArray(result&&result.peaks) ? result.peaks : [];
          duration = Math.max(MIN_CLIP_SEC,Number(result&&result.duration)||3);
        }

        if(pickedTarget==='replace-overlay'){
          const target = overlays.find(o=>String(o.id)===String(pickedReplaceId));
          if(!target) throw new Error('The selected overlay is no longer available.');
          target.type = type;
          target.src = mediaUrl;
          target.deviceMediaKey = mediaKey;
          target.deviceMediaName = pickedFile.name || '';
          target.deviceMediaSize = Number(pickedFile.size)||undefined;
          target.deviceMediaLastModified = Number(pickedFile.lastModified)||undefined;
          target.name = pickedFile.name || (type==='video'?'Video':type==='audio'?'Audio':'Image');
          target.sourceDuration = duration;
          target.sourceOffset = 0;
          target.sourceWidth = sourceWidth;target.sourceHeight = sourceHeight;
          target.thumbs = [];
          target.peaks = peaks;
          target.speed = Number(target.speed)||1;
          target.volume = (type==='video'||type==='audio') ? (target.volume==null?1:target.volume) : undefined;
          if(type==='video'||type==='image') target.transform = target.transform || {scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0};
          else target.transform = null;
          renderOverlayLayers();
          renderOverlayListPanel();
          renderFrame();
          persistProjectState();
        }else{
          addMediaOverlay({type,src:mediaUrl,name:pickedFile.name,duration,thumbs,peaks,sourceWidth,sourceHeight,appendAfterGroup:pickedAppendGroup,deviceMediaKey:mediaKey,deviceMediaInfo:{name:pickedFile.name,size:pickedFile.size,lastModified:pickedFile.lastModified}});
        }
        // Do not leave a project as a lightweight localStorage index until its full, latest
        // IndexedDB snapshot—including the new clip record and stable media key—is committed.
        if(typeof window.__ethvelleDeviceSaveNow==='function') await window.__ethvelleDeviceSaveNow();
        if(sessionOnly){
          showToast('Clip added for this session. Device storage is unavailable, so export a backup before leaving.',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:3600});
        }else if(!mediaKey){
          showToast('Clip added. This browser saved it in the project data; keep a backup of important work.',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:2600});
        }else{
          if(pickedTarget==='replace-overlay')showToast('Overlay media replaced and saved',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1500});
        }
      }catch(err){
        // Once an overlay references this key, keep both the Blob record and its live object URL
        // even if the full project snapshot write fails. Deleting it here would guarantee that
        // the just-added clip breaks as soon as the user refreshes.
        const alreadyAttached = !!(typeof overlays!=='undefined' && Array.isArray(overlays) && overlays.some(o=>o&&((mediaKey&&String(o.deviceMediaKey||'')===String(mediaKey))||(mediaUrl&&String(o.src||'')===String(mediaUrl)))));
        if(alreadyAttached){
          try{if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});}catch(_e){}
          try{if(typeof window.__ethvelleDeviceSaveNow==='function')await window.__ethvelleDeviceSaveNow();}catch(saveErr){console.error('Ethvelle project snapshot retry:',saveErr);}
          showToast(mediaKey?'Clip is still in the timeline. Its device save did not finish; export a backup before leaving.':'Clip is still in the timeline for this session. Export a backup before leaving because device storage is unavailable.',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:3800});
        }else{
          if(mediaUrl){try{URL.revokeObjectURL(mediaUrl);}catch(_e){}}
          if(mediaKey&&typeof window.__ethvelleDeleteStoredMediaFile==='function'){
            try{await window.__ethvelleDeleteStoredMediaFile(mediaKey);}catch(_e){}
          }
          showToast(err&&err.message ? err.message : 'Could not save this overlay. Please try again.',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2600});
        }
        console.error('Ethvelle persistent overlay import:',err);
      }
    })();
    return;
  }

  if(uploadTarget==='background'){
    // FEATURE (multiple sequential backgrounds): picking an image via the "Add background"
    // badge/empty-state now APPENDS a brand new background clip after the last one already
    // on the row (same "append after existing content" behavior addMediaOverlay already
    // gives Clips/Audio), instead of overwriting the single project-wide background in
    // place. Reads the file into a self-contained base64 data: URL first — never a
    // `url(blob:...)` object-URL — for the same reason documented on the overlay/media
    // branch below: blob URLs go stale the moment the page is closed/reopened, which used
    // to show up as a broken-image glyph on reload and in exported video.
    // UPDATED: close the Background panel the instant a file is chosen, BEFORE the async
    // FileReader finishes — previously it only closed inside onload, so the panel stayed
    // open (visible behind the freshly-added clip) for the whole read/decode. Now the panel
    // is gone immediately and the new 5s background simply appears on the timeline.
    closePanel();
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = ()=>{
      addBackgroundClip(`url(${reader.result})`);
      showToast('Background added — drag to move, pull the corner to resize', {duration:2200});
    };
    reader.onerror = ()=>{
      showToast("Couldn't read that image", {icon: toastIcons.info, duration:1800});
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  } else if(uploadTarget==='replace-background'){
    // Swaps ONLY the selected background clip's own image in place — keeps its
    // trimStart/trimEnd/transform untouched, mirroring how 'replace-overlay' below swaps a
    // Clips-row clip's content without moving or resizing it.
    const target = backgrounds.find(b=>b.id===replaceTargetId);
    if(!target){ closePanel(); return; }
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = ()=>{
      target.src = `url(${reader.result})`;
      replaceTargetId = null;
      refreshCurrentBgFromPlayhead();
      applyBackground();
      renderBackgroundTrack();
      showToast('Background replaced', {icon: toastIcons.check, duration:1600});
      closePanel();
      persistProjectState();
    };
    reader.onerror = ()=>{
      showToast("Couldn't read that image", {icon: toastIcons.info, duration:1800});
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  } else if(uploadTarget==='overlay' || uploadTarget==='media'){
    // BUGFIX ("the clips aren't saving after refreshing" — the actual saving bug): exactly
    // the same root cause the Background branch above already fixed once, but never applied
    // here. This used to hand addMediaOverlay a `URL.createObjectURL(file)` blob: URL as the
    // clip's `src`, and persistProjectState() writes that string verbatim into the saved
    // project. Blob URLs are only valid for the CURRENT page session — the instant the page
    // is closed/refreshed and the project is reopened, that exact blob: address no longer
    // resolves to anything. The clip's data (trimStart/trimEnd/name/thumbs) all survived in
    // localStorage just fine, so the clip still visibly showed up on the timeline after a
    // refresh — but its actual picture/video (src) was already dead, so it played back as a
    // blank/broken layer, reading as if "the clip didn't save". FIX: read the file into a
    // self-contained base64 data: URL first (identical technique to the Background fix
    // above), and use THAT as both the thumbnail-generation source and the persisted `src` —
    // nothing blob-based is ever stored. Video/audio can still be a large data: URL (same
    // localStorage-quota tradeoff the Background image already accepted — see
    // saveProjectsToStorage's own BUGFIX note), but a working clip that's occasionally too
    // big to save is a a strict improvement over a small clip that ALWAYS silently breaks.
    showToast(isVideo ? 'Loading video…' : 'Loading…', {icon: toastIcons.info, duration:1200});
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = ()=>{
      const dataUrl = reader.result;
      if(isVideo){
        generateVideoThumbnails(dataUrl, 24, 0, null).then(({duration,width,height})=>{
          addMediaOverlay({ type:'video', src:dataUrl, name:file.name, duration, thumbs:[], sourceWidth:width, sourceHeight:height, appendAfterGroup:uploadAppendGroup });
        });
      } else {
        // Image overlay: single thumbnail tiled across the clip body, like CapCut stickers/images.
        addMediaOverlay({ type:'image', src:dataUrl, name:file.name, duration:3, thumbs:[dataUrl], appendAfterGroup:uploadAppendGroup });
      }
    };
    reader.onerror = ()=>{
      showToast("Couldn't read that file", {icon: toastIcons.info, duration:1800});
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    closePanel();
  } else if(uploadTarget==='audio'){
    showToast('Loading audio…', {icon: toastIcons.info, duration:1200});
    // Same base64-instead-of-blob fix as the video/image branch above — see that BUGFIX note.
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = ()=>{
      const dataUrl = reader.result;
      generateAudioWaveform(dataUrl).then(({peaks, duration})=>{
        addMediaOverlay({ type:'audio', src:dataUrl, name:file.name, duration:duration||3, peaks, appendAfterGroup:uploadAppendGroup });
      });
    };
    reader.onerror = ()=>{
      showToast("Couldn't read that audio file", {icon: toastIcons.info, duration:1800});
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    closePanel();
  } else if(uploadTarget==='replace-overlay'){
    // Replace button (see replaceSelectedClip) — swaps the SRC/type/thumbnails of the
    // already-selected media clip in place, keeping its id/trimStart/trimEnd/transform
    // exactly where they were on the timeline. Unlike addMediaOverlay this never creates a
    // new clip or changes its position — only its underlying picture/video/audio content.
    const ov = overlays.find(o=>o.id===replaceTargetId);
    if(!ov){ closePanel(); return; }
    showToast(isVideo ? 'Loading video…' : isAudio ? 'Loading audio…' : 'Loading…', {icon: toastIcons.info, duration:1200});
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = ()=>{
      const dataUrl = reader.result;
      const finish = ({newType, thumbs, peaks, duration, sourceWidth, sourceHeight})=>{
        ov.type = newType;
        ov.src = dataUrl;
        ov.name = file.name;
        ov.sourceDuration = duration;
        if(newType==='video'){ov.sourceWidth=Number(sourceWidth)||0;ov.sourceHeight=Number(sourceHeight)||0;}
        ov.thumbs = newType==='video'?[]:(thumbs||[]);
        ov.peaks = peaks||[];
        // A shape/audio clip has no on-stage transform; an image/video does. Give it one if
        // it's newly gaining a visual and didn't have one before, same shape addMediaOverlay uses.
        if((newType==='video'||newType==='image') && !ov.transform) ov.transform = { scale:1, offsetX:0, offsetY:0, rotation:0 };
        renderOverlayLayers();
        renderOverlayListPanel();
        renderFrame();
        showToast('Replaced', {icon: toastIcons.check, duration:1200});
        persistProjectState();
      };
      if(isVideo){
        generateVideoThumbnails(dataUrl, 24, 0, null).then(({duration,width,height})=> finish({newType:'video', thumbs:[], duration, sourceWidth:width, sourceHeight:height}));
      } else if(isAudio){
        generateAudioWaveform(dataUrl).then(({peaks, duration})=> finish({newType:'audio', peaks, duration: duration||3}));
      } else {
        finish({newType:'image', thumbs:[dataUrl], duration: ov.sourceDuration||3});
      }
    };
    reader.onerror = ()=>{
      showToast("Couldn't read that file", {icon: toastIcons.info, duration:1800});
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    closePanel();
    replaceTargetId = null;
  } else {
    showToast('Upload received — layer support coming next version');
    closePanel();
  }
  uploadAppendGroup = null;
  e.target.value='';
});

// Unified entry point for adding uploaded media (image/video/audio) into the shared
// Clips or Audio group track. The current playhead decides the insertion point: before/left
// of a clip in its first half, after/right in its second half, or before the next clip in a gap.
// Every item in that track is then packed into one sequential lane.
function addMediaOverlay({type, src, name, duration, thumbs, peaks, appendAfterGroup, deviceMediaKey, deviceMediaInfo, sourceDuration, sourceOffset, sourceWidth, sourceHeight, adjustments, chromaKey, enhanceAmount, filterPreset}){
  const id = 'ov'+Date.now();
  const color = OVERLAY_COLORS[overlays.length % OVERLAY_COLORS.length];
  const clipLen = type==='video' ? Math.max(MIN_CLIP_SEC, Number(duration)||MIN_CLIP_SEC) : Math.max(MIN_CLIP_SEC,Math.min(Number(duration)||3,12)); // video clips keep their full source duration; only stills use the short default
  let startT=0;
  const groupKey = appendAfterGroup || (type==='audio' ? 'audio' : 'clips');
  const groupClips = overlays.filter(o=> (o.type==='audio'?'audio':(o.type==='text'?'text':'clips')) === groupKey);
  // Insert relative to the playhead, like a single-track editor: first half of the
  // clip => before/left of it; second half => after/right of it; a playhead sitting in
  // the gap before a clip => before that clip. Then repack the row sequentially.
  const orderedGroupClips=[...groupClips].sort((a,b)=>(Number(a.trimStart)||0)-(Number(b.trimStart)||0));
  const groupClipLengths=new Map(orderedGroupClips.map(o=>[String(o.id),Math.max(MIN_CLIP_SEC,(Number(o.trimEnd)||0)-(Number(o.trimStart)||0))]));
  let playheadTime=0;
  try{playheadTime=Math.max(0,Number(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:0))||0);}catch(_e){}
  let insertIndex=orderedGroupClips.length;
  for(let i=0;i<orderedGroupClips.length;i++){
    const existing=orderedGroupClips[i];
    const start=Number(existing.trimStart)||0;
    const len=groupClipLengths.get(String(existing.id))||MIN_CLIP_SEC;
    const end=start+len;
    // A playhead exactly at / just before a clip's leading edge is treated as "before it".
    if(playheadTime <= start+0.001){insertIndex=i;break;}
    if(playheadTime < end-0.001){
      insertIndex=playheadTime < start+len/2 ? i : i+1;
      break;
    }
  }
  startT=orderedGroupClips.slice(0,insertIndex).reduce((sum,o)=>sum+(groupClipLengths.get(String(o.id))||MIN_CLIP_SEC),0);
  const newClip = {
    id, name: name || (type==='video'?'Video':type==='audio'?'Audio':'Image'), color,
    type, src, deviceMediaKey: deviceMediaKey || undefined,
    deviceMediaName: deviceMediaInfo&&deviceMediaInfo.name || undefined,
    deviceMediaSize: deviceMediaInfo&&Number(deviceMediaInfo.size)||undefined,
    deviceMediaLastModified: deviceMediaInfo&&Number(deviceMediaInfo.lastModified)||undefined,
    sourceDuration: Number(sourceDuration)>0?Number(sourceDuration):(Number(duration)||clipLen), sourceOffset: Math.max(0,Number(sourceOffset)||0),
    sourceWidth: Number(sourceWidth)>0?Number(sourceWidth):0, sourceHeight: Number(sourceHeight)>0?Number(sourceHeight):0,
    trimStart: startT, trimEnd: startT + clipLen,
    adjustments: adjustments&&typeof adjustments==='object'?{...adjustments}:undefined,
    chromaKey: chromaKey&&typeof chromaKey==='object'?{enabled:!!chromaKey.enabled,color:chromaKey.color||'#00ff00',tolerance:Number(chromaKey.tolerance)||75,softness:Number(chromaKey.softness)||24}:undefined,
    enhanceAmount: Number(enhanceAmount)||0, filterPreset:filterPreset||'original',
    thumbs: thumbs||[], peaks: peaks||[], speed: 1,
    // FEATURE (volume control + audio extraction): every clip that can carry sound (video
    // or audio) now has its own volume multiplier, 0 (silent) to 2 (2x boost), defaulting
    // to 1 (unchanged) — see the Volume panel (renderVolumePanel/volumePanelSetVolume,
    // mirrors the existing Speed panel) and its live application in renderOverlayMedia/
    // renderAudioOverlayMedia below. An image/shape clip has nothing to carry volume on, so
    // stays undefined there (same pattern transform already uses for audio).
    volume: (type==='video'||type==='audio') ? 1 : undefined,
    // Own independent size/position/rotation (image/video overlays only — audio/shape have
    // no on-stage visual of their own to move). Lets the Transform panel adjust THIS clip's
    // picture on the canvas the same way it already adjusts the stickman — see
    // updateTransform's selectedClip-aware BUGFIX note.
    transform: (type==='video'||type==='image') ? { scale:1, offsetX:0, offsetY:0, rotation:0 } : null
  };
  const orderedWithNew=[...orderedGroupClips.slice(0,insertIndex),newClip,...orderedGroupClips.slice(insertIndex)];
  let groupCursor=0;
  orderedWithNew.forEach(item=>{
    const oldStart=Number(item.trimStart)||0;
    const oldKeyframeTimes=Array.isArray(item.ethvelleMediaKeyframes)?item.ethvelleMediaKeyframes.map(k=>Number(k&&k.t)||0):[];
    const len=item===newClip?clipLen:(groupClipLengths.get(String(item.id))||Math.max(MIN_CLIP_SEC,(Number(item.trimEnd)||0)-(Number(item.trimStart)||0)));
    item.trimStart=groupCursor;
    item.trimEnd=groupCursor+len;
    const movedBy=item.trimStart-oldStart;
    if(item!==newClip&&Math.abs(movedBy)>0.000001)shiftTimelineKeyframesOnce(item.ethvelleMediaKeyframes,oldKeyframeTimes,movedBy);
    groupCursor=item.trimEnd;
  });
  startT=Number(newClip.trimStart)||0;
  overlays.push(newClip);
  timelineDuration = Math.max(Number(timelineDuration)||0, groupCursor, startT+clipLen);
  renderOverlayLayers();
  syncTimelineInnerWidth();
  renderRuler();
  onClipSelected(newClip,'add');
  renderOverlayListPanel();
  // Immediately redraw the current playhead frame after the timeline is rearranged, so
  // the preview reflects the selected clip order without requiring a later play/scrub action.
  renderFrame();
  showToast((type==='video'?'Video':type==='audio'?'Audio':'Image')+(appendAfterGroup?' added to layer':' added'), {icon: toastIcons.check, duration:1200});
  persistProjectState();
}

// Background clips use the same midpoint insertion rule as every other timeline row:
// first half inserts before/left; second half inserts after/right; gaps insert before next.
function addBackgroundClip(src){
  const id='bg'+Date.now(),len=Math.max(MIN_CLIP_SEC,Number(BG_DEFAULT_DURATION)||3);
  const ordered=[...backgrounds].sort((a,b)=>(Number(a.trimStart)||0)-(Number(b.trimStart)||0));
  let playhead=0;try{playhead=Math.max(0,Number(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0);}catch(_e){}
  let at=ordered.length;
  for(let i=0;i<ordered.length;i++){
    const c=ordered[i],st=Number(c.trimStart)||0,l=Math.max(MIN_CLIP_SEC,(Number(c.trimEnd)||st+MIN_CLIP_SEC)-st),en=st+l;
    if(playhead<=st+.001){at=i;break;}
    if(playhead<en-.001){at=playhead<(st+en)/2?i:i+1;break;}
  }
  const clip={id,src,trimStart:0,trimEnd:len,transform:{scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0}};
  const arranged=[...ordered.slice(0,at),clip,...ordered.slice(at)];let cursor=0;
  arranged.forEach(c=>{
    const oldStart=Number(c.trimStart)||0;
    const oldKeyframeTimes=Array.isArray(c.ethvelleMediaKeyframes)?c.ethvelleMediaKeyframes.map(k=>Number(k&&k.t)||0):[];
    const l=c===clip?len:Math.max(MIN_CLIP_SEC,(Number(c.trimEnd)||0)-(Number(c.trimStart)||0));
    c.trimStart=cursor;c.trimEnd=cursor+l;
    const movedBy=c.trimStart-oldStart;
    if(c!==clip&&Math.abs(movedBy)>0.000001)shiftTimelineKeyframesOnce(c.ethvelleMediaKeyframes,oldKeyframeTimes,movedBy);
    cursor=c.trimEnd;
  });
  backgrounds.splice(0,backgrounds.length,...arranged);timelineDuration=Math.max(Number(timelineDuration)||0,cursor);
  renderBackgroundTrack();syncTimelineInnerWidth();renderRuler();
  onClipSelected(clip,'add');
  refreshCurrentBgFromPlayhead();applyBackground();updateSelBox();persistProjectState();return clip;
}

window.addEventListener('resize', ()=>{
  if(currentProject && document.getElementById('screen-editor').classList.contains('active')){
    setupCanvasStage(currentProject.ratio);
    renderFrame();
    renderTimelineClip();
    syncTimelineInnerWidth();
    renderRuler();
    syncPlayheadHeight();
    movePlayhead();
  }
  // Keep the pivot dot glued to its correct fractional position on the part image if the
  // pivot editor happens to be open across a resize/orientation change (its position is
  // computed from the image's on-screen box, which resize invalidates).
  if(document.getElementById('pivotEditorBackdrop').classList.contains('active')){
    const part = customBuilderParts[customBuilderActiveSlot];
    if(part){ positionPivotDot(part.pivotX, part.pivotY); positionContentBoxOutline(part); }
  }
});

/* ================= ADMIN-PUBLISHED PROMPTS / ASSETS / ANIMATION ACCESS ================= */
function getPromptLibrary(){return Array.isArray(siteContent.prompts)?siteContent.prompts.filter(p=>p&&p.builtin!==true&&p.adminOnly!==true&&p.source!=='builtin'):[];}
function getEditorAssets(){return Array.isArray(siteContent.assets)?siteContent.assets:[];}
// animationAccess[key] is a list of plan ids an admin checked for this animation (see the
// admin panel's multi-select Animation access rules). Older data saved before multi-plan
// support stored a single plan-id (or name, or 'free'/empty) as a bare string — normalized
// here into a one-item array so both shapes read identically.
function getAnimationAccessPlans(key){
  const map=siteContent.animationAccess||{}; const raw=map[key];
  const plans=typeof getPlans==='function'?getPlans():[];
  const free=plans.find(p=>Number(p.price)===0)||{id:'p_free',name:'Free',price:0};
  const ids=Array.isArray(raw)?raw.map(String).filter(Boolean):(raw==null||raw==='')?[]:[String(raw)];
  if(!ids.length)return [free];
  const resolved=ids.map(id=>{
    const normalized=id.toLowerCase();
    return plans.find(p=>String(p.id)===id) ||
      plans.find(p=>String(p.name||'').toLowerCase()===normalized) ||
      ((normalized==='free'||normalized==='p_free')?free:null);
  }).filter(Boolean);
  return resolved.length?resolved:[free];
}
// The badge/lock shown to a visitor is driven by the CHEAPEST plan an admin checked for this
// animation — e.g. if both Pro and Studio are checked, a Pro subscriber should already be able
// to use it rather than being told they need Studio.
function getAnimationRequiredPlan(key){
  const options=getAnimationAccessPlans(key);
  return options.slice().sort((a,b)=>Number(a.price||0)-Number(b.price||0))[0];
}
function isAnimationLocked(key){
  const required=getAnimationRequiredPlan(key);
  if(!required||Number(required.price)<=0)return false;
  const current=typeof getMyPlanData==='function'?getMyPlanData():{id:'p_free',name:'Free',price:0};
  // Treat the plans as tiers by price, so a higher tier (or an exact plan match) can use the
  // animation even if its id isn't literally in the checked list — the cheapest checked plan
  // sets the floor price a user's current plan must meet.
  const options=getAnimationAccessPlans(key);
  const directMatch=!!current && options.some(p=>String(p.id)===String(current.id));
  if(directMatch) return false;
  return !current || Number(current.price||0) < Number(required.price||0);
}
function openAnimationUpgrade(planId){
  goTo('payment');
  const p=(getPlans()||[]).find(x=>x.id===planId);
  if(p){const card=document.querySelector('#screen-payment .plan-card[data-plan="'+p.id+'"]');if(card&&typeof selectPlan==='function')selectPlan(card);}
  showToast('Upgrade your plan to add this animation. You can still preview it.',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2400});
}

  body.innerHTML=`
    <div class="ethvelle-audio-library-head">
      <button type="button" class="ethvelle-audio-library-back" onclick="returnToAudioPanel()" aria-label="Back to audio options"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M15 18l-6-6 6-6"/></svg></button>
      <div class="ethvelle-audio-library-heading">Audio Library</div>
    </div>
    <div class="editor-asset-toolbar">
      <div class="editor-asset-search-wrap" id="ethvelleAudioSearchWrap">
        <svg class="editor-asset-search-icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="10.8" cy="10.8" r="6.8"></circle><path d="m16 16 4.2 4.2"></path></svg>
        <input class="editor-asset-search" id="ethvelleAudioLibrarySearch" type="search" aria-label="Search audio files" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Search audio files…" oninput="filterEthvelleAudioLibrary(this.value)">
        <button type="button" class="editor-asset-search-clear" id="ethvelleAudioSearchClear" onclick="clearEthvelleAudioLibrarySearch()" aria-label="Clear audio search"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M7 7l10 10M17 7L7 17"/></svg></button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div id="ethvelleAudioLibraryResults" class="editor-asset-results"></div>`;

function renderEthvelleAudioLibraryResults(){
  const host=document.getElementById('ethvelleAudioLibraryResults');if(!host)return;
  const query=String(ethvelleAudioLibraryQuery||'').trim().toLocaleLowerCase();
  const audioAssets=getEditorAssets().filter(asset=>editorAssetCategory(asset)==='audio'&&String(asset.src||'').trim());
  const results=audioAssets.filter(asset=>{
    const text=[asset.name,asset.description,asset.keywords,asset.tags,asset.category,'audio'].flat().join(' ').toLocaleLowerCase();
    return !query||text.includes(query);
  });
  if(!results.length){
    host.innerHTML=audioAssets.length
      ? '<div class="ethvelle-audio-library-empty"><b>No matching audio files</b>Try another search keyword.</div>'
      : '<div class="ethvelle-audio-library-empty"><b>No audio files published yet</b>Audio files published under Admin → Assets → Audio will appear here. You can still use Upload to add audio from your device.</div>';
    return;
  }
  host.innerHTML=`<div class="ethvelle-audio-library-list">${results.map(asset=>{
    const id=String(asset.id||'');
    const src=String(asset.src||'');
    const name=String(asset.name||'Audio');
    const duration=Number(asset.duration)||0;
    const meta=duration>0?`Audio file · ${Math.floor(duration/60)}:${String(Math.floor(duration%60)).padStart(2,'0')}`:'Audio file';
    return `<article class="ethvelle-audio-library-card">
      <div class="ethvelle-audio-library-icon" aria-hidden="true">♫</div>
      <div class="ethvelle-audio-library-info">
        <span class="ethvelle-audio-library-name">${escapeHtml(name)}</span>
        <span class="ethvelle-audio-library-meta">${escapeHtml(meta)}</span>
        <audio controls preload="none" src="${escapeHtml(src)}" aria-label="Preview ${escapeHtml(name)}"></audio>
      </div>
      <button type="button" class="ethvelle-audio-library-add" onclick="addAdminAssetToProject('${escapeHtml(id)}')">Add to timeline</button>
    </article>`;
  }).join('')}</div>`;
}

const EDITOR_ASSET_FILTERS=[
  {id:'all',label:'All assets'},
  {id:'image',label:'Image clips'},
  {id:'video',label:'Video clips'},
  {id:'background-image',label:'Background images'},
  {id:'background-video',label:'Background videos'},
  {id:'audio',label:'Audio'},
  {id:'sticker',label:'Stickers'}
];
function editorAssetCategory(asset){
  const raw=String(asset?.category||asset?.type||'image').toLowerCase();
  if(['background-image','background-video','audio','font','image','video','sticker'].includes(raw))return raw;
  if(raw==='backgroundimage')return 'background-image';
  if(raw==='backgroundvideo')return 'background-video';
  return 'image';
}
function editorAssetLabel(asset){
  const category=editorAssetCategory(asset);
  return ({image:'Image clip / overlay',video:'Video clip', 'background-image':'Background image','background-video':'Background video',audio:'Audio clip',sticker:'Sticker'})[category]||'Asset';
}
function renderAssetsPanelBody(){
  return `<div class="editor-asset-toolbar">
      <div class="editor-asset-search-wrap${editorAssetSearchQuery?' has-query':''}">
        <svg class="editor-asset-search-icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="10.8" cy="10.8" r="6.8"></circle><path d="m16 16 4.2 4.2"></path></svg>
        <input class="editor-asset-search" id="editorAssetSearch" type="search" aria-label="Search assets" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Search images, videos, backgrounds, stickers…" value="${escapeHtml(editorAssetSearchQuery)}" oninput="filterEditorAssets(this.value)">
        <button type="button" class="editor-asset-search-clear" id="editorAssetSearchClear" onclick="clearEditorAssetSearch()" aria-label="Clear asset search"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M7 7l10 10M17 7L7 17"/></svg></button>
      </div>
      <div class="editor-asset-filters" id="editorAssetFilters">${EDITOR_ASSET_FILTERS.map(f=>`<button type="button" class="editor-asset-filter ${editorAssetCategoryFilter===f.id?'active':''}" data-filter="${f.id}" onclick="setEditorAssetCategory('${f.id}')">${f.label}</button>`).join('')}</div>
    </div>
    <div class="editor-asset-results" id="editorAssetResults"></div>`;
}
function renderAssetsPanel(){
  const el=document.getElementById('editorAssetLibrary');
  if(el){el.innerHTML=renderAssetsPanelBody();renderFilteredEditorAssets();}
}
function syncEditorAssetSearchClear(value){
  const wrap=document.querySelector('#screen-editor .editor-asset-search-wrap');
  if(wrap)wrap.classList.toggle('has-query',!!String(value||'').length);
}
function clearEditorAssetSearch(){
  const input=document.getElementById('editorAssetSearch');
  if(!input)return;
  input.value='';filterEditorAssets('');
  try{input.focus({preventScroll:true});}catch(_e){input.focus();}
}
function filterEditorAssets(value){editorAssetSearchQuery=String(value||'');syncEditorAssetSearchClear(editorAssetSearchQuery);renderFilteredEditorAssets();}
function setEditorAssetCategory(category){
  editorAssetCategoryFilter=EDITOR_ASSET_FILTERS.some(f=>f.id===category)?category:'all';
  document.querySelectorAll('#editorAssetFilters .editor-asset-filter').forEach(btn=>btn.classList.toggle('active',btn.dataset.filter===editorAssetCategoryFilter));
  renderFilteredEditorAssets();
}
function renderFilteredEditorAssets(){
  const host=document.getElementById('editorAssetResults');if(!host)return;
  const all=getEditorAssets();
  const q=editorAssetSearchQuery.trim().toLocaleLowerCase();
  const assets=all.filter(a=>{
    const category=editorAssetCategory(a);
    if(editorAssetCategoryFilter!=='all'&&category!==editorAssetCategoryFilter)return false;
    const searchable=[a.name,a.description,a.keywords,a.tags,a.fontFamily,category,editorAssetLabel(a)].flat().join(' ').toLocaleLowerCase();
    return !q||searchable.includes(q);
  });
  if(!assets.length){
    host.innerHTML=`<div class="editor-asset-empty">${all.length?'No matching assets found. Try another search or category.':'No admin assets published yet. Assets added in the Admin → Assets section will appear here.'}</div>`;
    return;
  }
  host.innerHTML=`<div class="editor-assets-grid">${assets.map(a=>{
    const category=editorAssetCategory(a),src=String(a.src||'');
    let preview='';
    if(category==='font'){ensureAdminFontLoaded(a);preview=`<div class="editor-asset-font-preview" style="font-family:${escapeHtml(a.fontFamily||a.name||'sans-serif')},sans-serif">Aa</div>`;}
    else if(category==='image'||category==='background-image')preview=src?`<img src="${escapeHtml(src)}" alt="" loading="lazy">`:'IMAGE';
    else if(category==='video'||category==='background-video')preview=`<div class="editor-asset-video">▶ VIDEO</div>`;
    else if(category==='audio')preview=`<div class="editor-asset-font-preview">♫</div>`;
    else preview='MEDIA';
    return `<button type="button" class="editor-asset-card" onclick="addAdminAssetToProject('${escapeHtml(a.id)}')"><span class="editor-asset-thumb">${preview}</span><span class="editor-asset-name">${escapeHtml(a.name||'Asset')}</span><span class="editor-asset-category">${escapeHtml(editorAssetLabel(a))}</span></button>`;
  }).join('')}</div>`;
}
function ensureAdminFontLoaded(assetOrFamily){
  const asset=typeof assetOrFamily==='object'&&assetOrFamily?assetOrFamily:getEditorAssets().find(a=>editorAssetCategory(a)==='font'&&String(a.fontFamily||a.name||'').toLowerCase()===String(assetOrFamily||'').toLowerCase());
  const family=String(asset?.fontFamily||asset?.name||assetOrFamily||'').trim();
  if(!family)return;
  const safeFamily=family.replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/"/g,'\\"');
  if(asset?.src){
    const src=String(asset.src);
    const key='ethvelle-font-'+family.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'-');
    if(document.getElementById(key))return;
    if(/\.(woff2?|ttf|otf)(?:[?#].*)?$/i.test(src)){
      const style=document.createElement('style');style.id=key;
      style.textContent='@font-face{font-family:"'+safeFamily+'";src:url("'+src.replace(/"/g,'%22')+'");font-display:swap;}';
      document.head.appendChild(style);
    }else if(/^https:\/\//i.test(src)){
      const link=document.createElement('link');link.id=key;link.rel='stylesheet';link.href=src;document.head.appendChild(link);
    }
  }else{
    const key='ethvelle-font-'+family.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'-');
    if(!document.getElementById(key)){
      const link=document.createElement('link');link.id=key;link.rel='stylesheet';
      const bengaliStylesheet=ETHVELLE_BN_WEB_FONT_CSS[family];
      link.href=bengaliStylesheet||('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family='+encodeURIComponent(family).replace(/%20/g,'+')+'&display=swap');
      document.head.appendChild(link);
    }
  }
}
function applyAdminFontAsset(asset){
  const family=String(asset.fontFamily||asset.name||'').trim();if(!family)return;
  ensureAdminFontLoaded(asset);
  const textClip=selectedTextOverlay();
  if(textClip){
    textClip.fontFamily=family;
    renderOverlayLayers();renderFrame();persistProjectState();
    showToast('Font applied to selected text',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1400});
    closePanel();
  }else{
    pendingAdminFontFamily=family;
    openPanel('text');
    showToast('Font selected — add text or apply it in the text editor',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:1800});
  }
}
function addAdminAssetToProject(id){
  const asset=getEditorAssets().find(a=>a.id===id);if(!asset){showToast('This asset is unavailable',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
  const category=editorAssetCategory(asset),src=String(asset.src||'');
  const videoOptions={sourceDuration:Number(asset.sourceDuration)||Number(asset.duration)||5,sourceOffset:Number(asset.sourceTrimStart)||0,adjustments:asset.adjustments||null,chromaKey:asset.chromaKey||null};
  const videoDuration=Math.max(MIN_CLIP_SEC,(Number(asset.sourceTrimEnd)||Number(asset.sourceDuration)||5)-(Number(asset.sourceTrimStart)||0));
  if(category==='font'){applyAdminFontAsset(asset);return;}
  if(!src){showToast('This asset has no media URL',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
  if(!currentProject){showToast('Open a project first',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
  if(category==='background-image'){
    addBackgroundClip(src);closePanel();showToast('Background image added',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1300});return;
  }
  if(category==='background-video'){
    addMediaOverlay({type:'video',src,name:asset.name||'Background video',duration:videoDuration,thumbs:[],peaks:[],...videoOptions});
    const clip=overlays.slice().reverse().find(o=>o.type==='video'&&o.src===src&&(o.name===(asset.name||'Background video')));
    if(clip){const idx=overlays.indexOf(clip);if(idx>0){overlays.splice(idx,1);overlays.unshift(clip);renderOverlayLayers();renderFrame();persistProjectState();}}
    closePanel();showToast('Background video added behind other media',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1400});return;
  }
  if(category==='audio'){
    addMediaOverlay({type:'audio',src,name:asset.name||'Audio',duration:Number(asset.duration)||5,thumbs:[],peaks:[]});
    const addedAudio=[...overlays].reverse().find(o=>o.type==='audio'&&o.src===src&&(o.name===(asset.name||'Audio')));
    if(addedAudio && typeof generateAudioWaveform==='function'){
      generateAudioWaveform(src).then(({peaks})=>{
        if(!overlays.includes(addedAudio))return;
        addedAudio.peaks=Array.from(peaks||[]);
        renderOverlayLayers();
        persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});
      }).catch(()=>{});
    }
    closePanel();return;
  }
  const type=(category==='video'||asset.type==='video')?'video':'image';
  addMediaOverlay({type,src,name:asset.name||'Asset',duration:type==='video'?videoDuration:(Number(asset.duration)||5),thumbs:[],peaks:[],...(type==='video'?videoOptions:{})});
  closePanel();
}
function renderPromptGrid(targetId,prompts){
  const wrap=document.getElementById(targetId);if(!wrap)return;
  if(!prompts.length){wrap.innerHTML='<div class="home-prompt-empty">No prompts published yet.</div>';return;}
  wrap.innerHTML=prompts.map(p=>{
    const image=p.templateImage||p.referenceImage;
    const thumb=image?`<img src="${escapeHtml(image)}" alt="${escapeHtml(p.title||'Prompt')}" loading="lazy">`:'PROMPT';
    const imageTypes=[p.referenceImage?'Reference image':'',p.templateImage?'Template image':''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
    const desc=p.guidance||p.prompt||'Tap to view and copy this prompt.';
    return `<button class="home-prompt-card" onclick="openPromptDetails('${escapeHtml(p.id)}')"><span class="home-prompt-thumb">${thumb}</span><span class="home-prompt-name">${escapeHtml(p.title||'Prompt')}</span>${imageTypes?`<span class="home-prompt-desc">${escapeHtml(imageTypes)}</span>`:''}<span class="home-prompt-desc">${escapeHtml(desc)}</span></button>`;
  }).join('');
}
let promptSearchQuery='';
function renderPromptLibrary(){
  const allPrompts=getPromptLibrary();
  const q=String(promptSearchQuery||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const prompts=q?allPrompts.filter(p=>[p.title,p.guidance,p.prompt,p.referenceImage?'Reference image':'',p.templateImage?'Template image':''].filter(Boolean).join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q)):allPrompts;
  const wrap=document.getElementById('keyframePromptGrid');
  if(q && !prompts.length){if(wrap)wrap.innerHTML='<div class="home-prompt-empty">No prompts match your search. Try another keyword.</div>';return;}
  renderPromptGrid('keyframePromptGrid',prompts);
}
