let currentPromptToCopy='';
function openPromptDetails(id){
  const p=getPromptLibrary().find(x=>x.id===id);if(!p)return;currentPromptToCopy=String(p.prompt||'');
  document.getElementById('promptDetailTitle').textContent=p.title||'Prompt';
  const html=[];
  if(p.guidance)html.push(`<div class="prompt-detail-label">Guidance</div><div class="prompt-detail-copy">${escapeHtml(p.guidance)}</div>`);
  html.push(`<div class="prompt-detail-label">Prompt</div><div class="prompt-detail-copy" id="promptBodyText">${escapeHtml(p.prompt||'')}</div>`);
  const imageDownloadButton=(url,kind,title)=>`<button type="button" class="prompt-image-download" data-src="${escapeHtml(url)}" data-kind="${kind}" data-title="${escapeHtml(title||'Ethvelle') }" aria-label="Download ${kind} image" title="Download image" onclick="event.stopPropagation();downloadPromptImage(this)"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 3v12m-5-5 5 5 5-5M5 16v4h14v-4"/></svg></button>`;
  if(p.referenceImage)html.push(`<div class="prompt-detail-label">Reference image</div><div class="prompt-detail-img-wrap"><img class="prompt-detail-img" src="${escapeHtml(p.referenceImage)}" alt="Reference image">${imageDownloadButton(p.referenceImage,'reference',p.title)}</div>`);
  if(p.templateImage)html.push(`<div class="prompt-detail-label">Template image</div><div class="prompt-detail-img-wrap"><img class="prompt-detail-img" src="${escapeHtml(p.templateImage)}" alt="Template image">${imageDownloadButton(p.templateImage,'template',p.title)}</div>`);
  document.getElementById('promptDetailBody').innerHTML=html.join('');document.getElementById('promptDetailBackdrop').classList.add('active');
}
async function downloadPromptImage(button){
  if(!button||button.disabled)return;
  const url=button.dataset.src||'';
  if(!url){showToast('Image is unavailable',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
  const kind=button.dataset.kind==='template'?'template':'reference';
  const title=String(button.dataset.title||'Ethvelle').normalize('NFKD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]+/g,'-').replace(/^-+|-+$/g,'').slice(0,55)||'Ethvelle';
  const baseName=`${title}-${kind}-image`;
  const originalText=button.querySelector('span')?.textContent||'Download';
  button.disabled=true;
  if(button.querySelector('span'))button.querySelector('span').textContent='Downloading…';
  try{
    const response=await fetch(url,{mode:'cors',credentials:'omit'});
    if(!response.ok)throw new Error('Image request failed');
    const blob=await response.blob();
    if(!blob.size)throw new Error('Empty image');
    const mime=(blob.type||'').split(';')[0].toLowerCase();
    const mimeExt={'image/jpeg':'jpg','image/jpg':'jpg','image/png':'png','image/webp':'webp','image/gif':'gif','image/avif':'avif','image/bmp':'bmp','image/svg+xml':'svg'};
    let ext=mimeExt[mime]||'';
    if(!ext){try{const path=new URL(url,location.href).pathname;const match=path.match(/\.([a-z0-9]{2,5})$/i);ext=match?match[1].toLowerCase():'jpg';}catch(e){ext='jpg';}}
    const objectUrl=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link=document.createElement('a');link.href=objectUrl;link.download=`${baseName}.${ext}`;link.style.display='none';
    document.body.appendChild(link);link.click();link.remove();
    setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl),1500);
    showToast('Image downloaded',{icon:toastIcons.check});
  }catch(error){
    // Some image hosts block cross-origin downloads. Open the source directly as a fallback.
    const link=document.createElement('a');link.href=url;link.target='_blank';link.rel='noopener noreferrer';link.download=`${baseName}.jpg`;link.style.display='none';
    document.body.appendChild(link);link.click();link.remove();
    showToast('Opening image — use Save image if download does not start',{icon:toastIcons.info});
  }finally{
    button.disabled=false;
    if(button.querySelector('span'))button.querySelector('span').textContent=originalText;
  }
}
function closePromptDetails(){document.getElementById('promptDetailBackdrop').classList.remove('active');}
function copyPromptText(){
  const text=String(currentPromptToCopy||'');if(!text){showToast('No prompt text to copy',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
  if(navigator.clipboard&&navigator.clipboard.writeText){navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(()=>showToast('Prompt copied',{icon:toastIcons.check})).catch(()=>fallbackCopyPrompt(text));}
  else fallbackCopyPrompt(text);
}
function fallbackCopyPrompt(text){const ta=document.createElement('textarea');ta.value=text;ta.style.position='fixed';ta.style.opacity='0';document.body.appendChild(ta);ta.select();let ok=false;try{ok=document.execCommand('copy');}catch(e){}ta.remove();showToast(ok?'Prompt copied':'Select and copy the prompt manually',{icon:ok?toastIcons.check:toastIcons.info});}

/* Location-based in-app notification popups. Existing inbox notifications remain available too. */
let locationPopupNotificationId=null;
function notifPopupSeenKey(uid){return 'riggy.notifPopupSeen.'+uid;}
function getNotifPopupSeen(){return currentUser?readIdList(notifPopupSeenKey(currentUser.uid)):[];}
function activeScreenId(){const s=document.querySelector('.screen.active');return s?(s.id||'').replace(/^screen-/,''):'';}
function normalizeNotificationScreen(value){
  const v=String(value==null?'':value).trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z]/g,'');
  if(!v) return '';
  if(v==='all'||v==='everywhere'||v==='allscreens') return 'all';
  if(v.startsWith('home')||v.includes('homepage')||v.includes('homescreen')) return 'home';
  if(v.startsWith('project')||v.includes('projectscreen')) return 'projects';
  if(v.startsWith('profile')||v.includes('profilescreen')) return 'profile';
  if(v.startsWith('payment')||v.includes('paymentscreen')) return 'payment';
  return v.replace(/screen$/,'');
}
function notificationShowTargets(value){
  if(Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(normalizeNotificationScreen).filter(Boolean);
  if(typeof value==='string' && value.trim()) return value.split(/[;,|]/).map(normalizeNotificationScreen).filter(Boolean);
  if(value && typeof value==='object') return Object.keys(value).filter(k=>value[k]===true || value[k]===1 || value[k]==='true').map(normalizeNotificationScreen).filter(Boolean);
  return [];
}
function maybeShowLocationNotification(screenName){
  if(!currentUser||!screenName||!Array.isArray(myNotifications))return;
  const active=normalizeNotificationScreen(screenName);
  const backdrop=document.getElementById('notificationPopupBackdrop');if(!backdrop||backdrop.classList.contains('active'))return;
  const titleEl=document.getElementById('notificationPopupTitle'), bodyEl=document.getElementById('notificationPopupBody');
  if(!titleEl||!bodyEl)return;
  const seen=getNotifPopupSeen();
  const candidate=visibleNotifications().find(n=>{
    if(seen.includes(n.id)) return false;
    const rawTarget = n.showOn ?? n.showOnScreens ?? n.popupOn ?? n.displayOn ?? n.screen ?? n.location ?? n.showLocation ?? n.popupLocation ?? n.displayLocation;
    const targets=notificationShowTargets(rawTarget);
    // Older admin notifications omit their placement field; they are treated as Home notifications.
    // "All screens" should be eligible everywhere, regardless of its original casing/spacing.
    return targets.length ? (targets.includes('all') || targets.includes(active)) : active==='home';
  });
  if(!candidate)return;
  locationPopupNotificationId=candidate.id;
  titleEl.textContent=candidate.title||candidate.subject||'Notification';
  bodyEl.textContent=candidate.body||candidate.message||candidate.description||'';
  backdrop.classList.add('active');
  seen.push(candidate.id);writeIdList(notifPopupSeenKey(currentUser.uid),seen);
}
function closeLocationNotification(){const el=document.getElementById('notificationPopupBackdrop');if(el)el.classList.remove('active');locationPopupNotificationId=null;setTimeout(()=>maybeShowLocationNotification(activeScreenId()),180);}
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.visibilityState==='visible')setTimeout(()=>maybeShowLocationNotification(activeScreenId()),220);});
window.addEventListener('focus',()=>setTimeout(()=>maybeShowLocationNotification(activeScreenId()),180));

/* ================= SITE CONTENT (read-only mirror of admin-published data) =================
   Banners / example animations / subscription plans are managed from the separate admin
   panel file (riggy-admin-panel.html), which writes them to Firestore under
   siteContent/main. This app only ever READS that document — it has no admin login and
   cannot edit this content. Falls back to localStorage (cached from the last successful
   read) and finally to the built-in defaults below if Firestore isn't reachable, so the
   Home/Payment screens always have something to show. */
const SITE_CONTENT_CACHE_KEY = 'riggy.siteContent.cache.v1';
const SPLIT_SITE_CONTENT_KEYS = ['promoPopups','prompts','assets','models','animationAccess'];
const splitSiteContentLoaded = { promoPopups:false, prompts:false, assets:false, models:false, animationAccess:false };
const splitSiteContentExists = { promoPopups:false, prompts:false, assets:false, models:false, animationAccess:false };
let splitSiteContentUnsubs = [];
// customAnimPresets: admin-authored entries in the SAME shape as the built-in animPresets
// object below ({ name, duration, keyframes:[{t, pose:{...}}] }), keyed by a slug the admin
// panel generates from the name. Kept as its own array of {key, preset} pairs in Firestore
// (not merged into animPresets directly in storage) so the admin panel can list/edit/delete
// them independently of the built-ins — applyCustomAnimPresets() below is what actually
// merges them into the live animPresets object the Animate picker and playback read from.
let siteContent = { banners:null, animations:null, plans:null, tiles:null, tutorials:null, contactInfo:null, creditSettings:null, promoPopups:null, customAnimPresets:null, prompts:[], assets:[], models:[], animationAccess:{} };
try{
  const raw = localStorage.getItem(SITE_CONTENT_CACHE_KEY);
  if(raw) siteContent = JSON.parse(raw);
}catch(e){}
// The earlier app script initializes this list without touching siteContent. Hydrate it only
// after siteContent has been declared and the local cache has been parsed safely.
publishedModelAssets = Array.isArray(siteContent.models) ? siteContent.models.map(m=>({...m,origin:'admin'})) : [];

// Tutorials start empty (not a handful of placeholder cards like banners/tiles/animations
// do) — until the admin adds real videos from the admin panel's Tutorials page, the screen
// just shows its own empty state rather than fake-looking demo cards.
function defaultTutorials(){ return []; }
// Promo popups: empty by default, same reasoning as Tutorials above — nothing shows until
// the admin actually configures one. See the admin panel's Promo popup page for the shape.
function defaultPromoPopups(){ return []; }
function getBanners(){ return siteContent.banners || defaultBanners(); }
function getAnimations(){ return siteContent.animations || defaultAnimations(); }
function getPlans(){
  const source=Array.isArray(siteContent.plans)&&siteContent.plans.length?siteContent.plans:defaultPlans();
  return source.map(plan=>{
    if(!plan)return plan;
    const isFree=Number(plan.price)===0||['free','p_free'].includes(String(plan.id||'').toLowerCase())||String(plan.name||'').trim().toLowerCase()==='free';
    if(!isFree)return plan;
    const otherFeatures=(Array.isArray(plan.features)?plan.features:[]).filter(feature=>!/(projects?|exports?|(?:480|720|1080|1440|2160)p|\b4k\b)/i.test(String(feature||'')));
    return {...plan,features:['3 projects','720p export',...otherFeatures],maxQuality:720,unlimitedProjects:false,maxProjects:3,unlimitedExports:false};
  });
}
function defaultCreditSettings(){ return { enabled:true, exportPerMinute:{480:20,720:40,1080:60,1440:80,2160:100} }; }
function getCreditSettings(){
  const defaults = defaultCreditSettings();
  const saved = siteContent && siteContent.creditSettings || {};
  return { enabled:saved.enabled !== false, exportPerMinute:{...defaults.exportPerMinute,...(saved.exportPerMinute||{})} };
}
function getPlanCreditGrant(plan){
  if(!plan) return 0;
  if(plan.creditsPerCycle != null && Number.isFinite(Number(plan.creditsPerCycle))) return Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(plan.creditsPerCycle)));
  if(String(plan.id)==='p_pro') return 1000;
  if(String(plan.id)==='p_studio') return 4000;
  return 0;
}
function getExportCreditRate(quality){ const settings=getCreditSettings(); return Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(settings.exportPerMinute[String(quality)] ?? settings.exportPerMinute[quality])||0)); }
function calculateExportCreditCost(quality,durationSeconds){
  const settings=getCreditSettings();
  if(!settings.enabled) return 0;
  const seconds=Math.max(0,Number(durationSeconds)||0);
  const rate=getExportCreditRate(quality);
  if(seconds<=0 || rate<=0) return 0;
  return Math.max(1,Math.ceil(seconds*rate/60));
}
function getPromoPopups(){ return siteContent.promoPopups || defaultPromoPopups(); }
// FEATURE (admin-authored Custom Animations, published from the admin panel's new
// "Custom animations" page): merges every admin-published preset into the live animPresets
// object so they show up in the Animate picker exactly like idle/walk/wave/jump do, for
// every user. Only ever ADDS/overwrites admin-authored keys (each one prefixed 'custom_' by
// the admin panel when it creates them, so a custom preset can never collide with or
// shadow a built-in key) — never deletes from animPresets, so removing a custom preset in
// the admin panel just means its key stops being re-applied on the next sync; a project
// that already has a clip using a deleted custom preset keeps working until the user picks
// something else (same "orphaned animKey" tolerance evalPreset/animPresets[clip.animKey]
// already has to handle anyway if a key is ever missing).
function applyCustomAnimPresets(){
  // Shape published by the admin panel's Custom Animations page: a flat array of
  // { id, key, name, duration, keyframes }, one per preset — key is always 'custom_'-prefixed
  // (see the admin panel's slugifyAnimName) and keyframes are already in radians (the admin
  // panel converts from the degrees an admin types in before publishing).
  const list = siteContent.customAnimPresets;
  if(!Array.isArray(list)) return;
  list.forEach(entry=>{
    if(!entry || !entry.key || !entry.name) return;
    if(!String(entry.key).startsWith('custom_')) return; // defensive: admin panel always sets this prefix
    if(!Array.isArray(entry.keyframes) || !entry.keyframes.length) return; // malformed — skip, don't crash the picker
    animPresets[entry.key] = { name:String(entry.name), duration: Number(entry.duration)>0 ? Number(entry.duration) : 1, keyframes: entry.keyframes };
  });
}
function defaultContactInfo(){ return { email:'support@ethvelle.com', whatsapp:'' }; }
function getContactInfo(){ return Object.assign({}, defaultContactInfo(), siteContent.contactInfo || {}); }
// MIGRATION: the built-in Character/Keyframe tiles used to open the ratio picker
// (actionType:'ratio') like Video does. They now have real destinations of their own (the
// Models tab / the new Keyframe info screen — see defaultTiles). A site that saved tiles to
// Firestore BEFORE this change (any edit to any tile re-saves the whole array — see saveTile
// in the admin panel) would otherwise keep reopening the ratio picker forever, since
// Firestore's stored copy wins over defaultTiles(). Patched here, once per read, rather than
// in Firestore directly, so it self-heals on every device without a manual admin re-save.
function migrateLegacyTiles(tiles){
  if(!Array.isArray(tiles)) return tiles;
  return tiles.map(t=>{
    if(!t || !t.builtin || t.actionType !== 'ratio') return t;
    if(t.id === 't_default_1') return { ...t, actionType:'screen', actionValue:'models' };
    if(t.id === 't_default_4') return { ...t, actionType:'screen', actionValue:'keyframeInfo' };
    return t;
  });
}
function getTiles(){ return migrateLegacyTiles(siteContent.tiles) || defaultTiles(); }
function getTutorials(){ return siteContent.tutorials || defaultTutorials(); }

// Resolves a banner/animation/tile tap: either navigate in-app, open the new-project ratio
// picker (the "create" tiles' original behavior), or open an external link.
function runAdminAction(actionType, actionValue){
  if(actionType === 'link' && actionValue){
    window.open(actionValue, '_blank', 'noopener');
  } else if(actionType === 'ratio'){
    openRatioModal();
  } else if(actionValue){
    goTo(actionValue);
  }
}

/* ================= PROMO POPUP =================
   Shows at most one promo popup per app session (not per visit to Home — once dismissed or
   acted on, it won't reappear until the page is fully reloaded), picked from whichever active
   popups exist, after its own configured delay. If several are active, the one with the
   shortest delay fires first; the rest are skipped for this session rather than queued, so the
   person is never hit with a second popup right after closing the first.
   window.activePromoDiscount is read by computePlanDiscount() (Payment screen) once a
   "Give a discount" popup is tapped — { planId, type, value, label } or null. */
window.activePromoDiscount = null;
const ETHVELLE_PROMO_ATTRIBUTION_KEY = 'ethvelle.promoAttribution.v1';
function setPromoAttribution(pp){
  if(!pp || !pp.id) return;
  try{ sessionStorage.setItem(ETHVELLE_PROMO_ATTRIBUTION_KEY, JSON.stringify({popupId:String(pp.id), clickedAt:Date.now()})); }catch(e){}
}
function getPromoAttribution(){
  try{
    const raw=sessionStorage.getItem(ETHVELLE_PROMO_ATTRIBUTION_KEY);
    if(!raw) return null;
    const data=JSON.parse(raw);
    if(!data || !data.popupId) return null;
    // Attribution is tab/session scoped and expires after four hours to avoid stale cross-journey credit.
    if(data.clickedAt && Date.now()-Number(data.clickedAt)>4*60*60*1000){ sessionStorage.removeItem(ETHVELLE_PROMO_ATTRIBUTION_KEY); return null; }
    return {popupId:String(data.popupId)};
  }catch(e){ return null; }
}
function clearPromoAttribution(){ try{ sessionStorage.removeItem(ETHVELLE_PROMO_ATTRIBUTION_KEY); }catch(e){} }
function withPromoAttribution(details){
  const base=details && typeof details==='object' ? details : {};
  const attribution=getPromoAttribution();
  return attribution ? {...base, popupId:attribution.popupId, source:'promo_popup'} : base;
}
function trackPromoAttributedEvent(kind, details){
  const attribution=getPromoAttribution();
  if(!attribution) return;
  trackEvent(kind, {...(details && typeof details==='object' ? details : {}), popupId:attribution.popupId, source:'promo_popup'});
}
let promoPopupShownThisSession = false;
let promoPopupTimer = null;
let promoPopupCurrent = null;
// When Firestore is available, do not schedule a popup from the stale local cache. Wait for
// the first server-confirmed snapshot, then schedule from the newest published data. This
// prevents the old popup/offer appearing on the first visit after an admin update.
let promoPopupContentServerConfirmed = !(firebaseReady && fbDb);
function promoPopupVisibleOnScreen(pp,screenName){
  const targets=Array.isArray(pp&&pp.showOn)?pp.showOn:(typeof (pp&&pp.showOn)==='string'?[pp.showOn]:['home']);
  return targets.includes('all') || targets.includes(screenName);
}
// Audience scope is independent from the action/discount target. Legacy popups that predate
// audiencePlans remain visible to everyone; newly configured popups can target guests, Free
// users, or one/more paid plan IDs.
function promoPopupVisibleToCurrentUser(pp){
  const targets = Array.isArray(pp && pp.audiencePlans) ? pp.audiencePlans : ['__all__'];
  if(targets.includes('__all__')) return true;
  if(!currentUser) return targets.includes('guest');
  const plans = getPlans();
  const activePlan = plans.find(p=>p.id===myActivePlanId) || plans.find(p=>Number(p.price)===0);
  if(!activePlan || Number(activePlan.price)<=0) return targets.includes('free');
  return targets.includes(activePlan.id);
}
function wirePromoPopups(forceReschedule=false, screenOverride=null){
  if(promoPopupShownThisSession || !promoPopupContentServerConfirmed) return;
  if(promoPopupTimer){
    if(!forceReschedule) return;
    clearTimeout(promoPopupTimer); promoPopupTimer=null;
  }
  const screenName=screenOverride || activeScreenId() || 'home';
  const eligible=getPromoPopups().filter(pp=>pp&&pp.active!==false&&safeImageUrl(pp.image)&&promoPopupVisibleOnScreen(pp,screenName)&&promoPopupVisibleToCurrentUser(pp));
  if(!eligible.length)return;
  const soonest=eligible.slice().sort((a,b)=>(Number(a.delaySeconds)||0)-(Number(b.delaySeconds)||0))[0];
  promoPopupTimer=setTimeout(()=>{
    promoPopupTimer=null;
    if(promoPopupShownThisSession)return;
    const currentScreen=activeScreenId();
    const latest=getPromoPopups().filter(pp=>pp&&pp.active!==false&&safeImageUrl(pp.image)&&promoPopupVisibleOnScreen(pp,currentScreen)&&promoPopupVisibleToCurrentUser(pp))
      .slice().sort((a,b)=>(Number(a.delaySeconds)||0)-(Number(b.delaySeconds)||0))[0];
    if(latest) showPromoPopup(latest);
    else wirePromoPopups(true,currentScreen); // audience/screen may have changed while the delay was running
  },Math.max(0,Number(soonest.delaySeconds)||0)*1000);
}
function showPromoPopup(pp){
  promoPopupCurrent = pp;
  promoPopupShownThisSession = true;
  trackEvent('promo_popup_shown', { popupId:pp && pp.id, screen:trackingActiveScreen() });
  document.getElementById('promoPopupImg').src = safeImageUrl(pp.image);
  document.getElementById('promoPopupBackdrop').classList.add('active');
}
function closePromoPopup(){
  document.getElementById('promoPopupBackdrop').classList.remove('active');
}
function handlePromoPopupTap(){
  const pp = promoPopupCurrent;
  closePromoPopup();
  if(!pp) return;
  setPromoAttribution(pp);
  trackEvent('promo_popup_click', { popupId:pp.id, screen:trackingActiveScreen(), source:'promo_popup' });
  if(pp.actionType === 'discount' && pp.discountPlanId){
    // A promo can target one paid plan or every paid plan (__all_paid__). The promo is
    // intentionally kept for the rest of this page session so the prices stay consistent
    // while the user browses the Payment screen and proceeds to checkout.
    // Older saved popups predate discountScope; treat them as all-plan promos so a
    // previously configured home popup no longer discounts only its legacy target plan.
    const applyToAll = pp.discountPlanId === '__all_paid__' || pp.discountScope !== 'specific';
    const selectionPlanId = pp.discountPlanId !== '__all_paid__' ? pp.discountPlanId : '';
    window.activePromoDiscount = {
      planId: applyToAll ? '__all_paid__' : pp.discountPlanId,
      applyToAll,
      type: pp.discountType || 'percent',
      value: Number(pp.discountValue) || 0,
      label: 'Promo discount'
    };
    _plansSig = null;
    goTo('payment');
    // Navigation alone does not redraw the plans; rebuild them now so the promo prices are
    // visible immediately instead of waiting for an unrelated Firestore snapshot.
    renderPaymentPlans();
    if(selectionPlanId){
      const card = document.querySelector('#screen-payment .plan-card[data-plan="'+selectionPlanId+'"]');
      if(card) selectPlan(card);
    }
  } else {
    runAdminAction(pp.actionType, pp.actionValue);
  }
}

/* ---------------- SAFE HELPERS ---------------- */
// Only ever put http(s) URLs or inline image data URLs into an <img src>.
function safeImageUrl(u){
  return (typeof u === 'string' && /^(https?:\/\/|data:image\/)/i.test(u)) ? u : '';
}
// Re-rendering replaces the whole strip, which snaps the swipe position back to slide 1 and
// makes every picture flash. Firestore snapshots fire more often than the content actually
// changes, so each renderer remembers what it last drew and skips identical data.
let _bannerSig = null, _animSig = null, _plansSig = null, _tutorialsSig = null;

// Banner / example-card taps go through ONE delegated listener reading data-* attributes.
// (The old inline onclick="…runAdminAction('screen', "home")" put JSON's double quotes inside a
// double-quoted attribute, which cut the attribute short — so taps never fired.)
function wireContentTaps(){
  ['homeBannerScroll','homeVideoScroll','homeTileRow','tutorialGrid'].forEach(id=>{
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if(!el || el.dataset.tapsWired) return;
    el.dataset.tapsWired = '1';
    el.addEventListener('click', e=>{
      const hit = e.target.closest('[data-atype]');
      if(!hit || !el.contains(hit)) return;
      runAdminAction(hit.dataset.atype, hit.dataset.avalue || '');
    });
  });
}

// Image load / error handling for banner <img>s. A failed load is retried once (a CDN that is
// briefly slow shouldn't leave a blank banner) before showing the fallback panel.
function bannerImgLoaded(img){
  const box = img.closest('.home-banner');
  if(box){ box.classList.remove('failed'); box.classList.add('loaded'); }
}
function bannerImgFailed(img){
  const box = img.closest('.home-banner');
  const url = img.getAttribute('src') || '';
  if(!img.dataset.retried && /^https?:/i.test(url)){
    img.dataset.retried = '1';
    setTimeout(()=>{ img.src = url + (url.indexOf('?') > -1 ? '&' : '?') + 'r=' + Date.now(); }, 2500);
    return;
  }
  if(box){ box.classList.remove('loaded'); box.classList.add('failed'); }
}

/* ---------------- RENDER: HOME BANNER ---------------- */
// Homepage banners are supplied by the admin panel; this HTML contains no built-in promotional banner.
function renderHomeBanners(){
  const scroller = document.getElementById('homeBannerScroll');
  const dotsWrap = document.getElementById('homeBannerDots');
  if(!scroller || !dotsWrap) return;
  // Render only banners published from the admin panel. Do not inject a default hero or demo slides.
  const banners = (getBanners() || []).filter(b=>b);
  const sig = JSON.stringify(banners);
  if(sig === _bannerSig) return;
  _bannerSig = sig;
  if(!banners.length){
    scroller.innerHTML = '';
    dotsWrap.innerHTML = '';
    scroller.style.display = 'none';
    dotsWrap.style.display = 'none';
    return;
  }
  scroller.style.display = 'flex';
  dotsWrap.style.display = banners.length > 1 ? 'flex' : 'none';

  scroller.innerHTML = banners.map(b=>{
    const img = safeImageUrl(b.image);
    const attrs = ` data-atype="${escapeHtml(b.actionType||'screen')}" data-avalue="${escapeHtml(b.actionValue||'')}"`;
    // The admin can switch the CTA off. Missing actionEnabled remains enabled for older banners.
    const showAction = b.actionEnabled !== false && b.showAction !== false;
    const actionText = String(b.actionText || b.buttonText || b.buttonLabel || b.ctaText || b.actionLabel || 'Explore').trim() || 'Explore';
    if(img){
      const actionAttrs = attrs;
      const actionButton = showAction ? `<button type="button" class="home-banner-image-action"${actionAttrs} aria-label="${escapeHtml(actionText)}">
            <span>${escapeHtml(actionText)}</span>
            <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 12h14M13 6l6 6-6 6"/></svg>
          </button>` : '';
      return `<div class="home-banner-slide">
        <div class="home-banner has-image">
          <img src="${escapeHtml(img)}" alt="${escapeHtml(b.title||'Banner')}" decoding="async"
               onload="bannerImgLoaded(this)" onerror="bannerImgFailed(this)">
          <div class="home-banner-fail"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="3" y="3" width="18" height="18" rx="2"/><circle cx="9" cy="9" r="2"/><path d="M21 15l-5-5L5 21"/></svg></div>
          ${actionButton}
        </div>
      </div>`;
    }
    return `<div class="home-banner-slide"${attrs}>
      <div class="home-banner">
        <div class="home-banner-tag">${escapeHtml(b.tag||'')}</div>
        <h2>${escapeHtml(b.title||'')}</h2>
        <p>${escapeHtml(b.desc||'')}</p>
        <button class="home-banner-btn"${attrs}>
          View
          <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#0A0A0C" stroke-width="2.6" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M5 12h14M13 6l6 6-6 6"/></svg>
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }).join('');
  dotsWrap.innerHTML = banners.map((_,i)=>`<span${i===0?' class="active"':''} data-i="${i}"></span>`).join('');
  scroller.scrollLeft = 0;

  // Pictures that finished (or failed) before their onload/onerror could run — e.g. served
  // straight from cache — would otherwise stay invisible, so settle them explicitly.
  scroller.querySelectorAll('.home-banner.has-image img').forEach(img=>{
    if(img.complete){ (img.naturalWidth > 0) ? bannerImgLoaded(img) : bannerImgFailed(img); }
  });
  wireBannerDots();
}

/* ---------------- RENDER: "WHAT WOULD YOU CREATE" TILES ----------------
   Higgsfield-style tilted photo tiles with a deep-blue neon 3D border when the admin has
   uploaded an image; falls back to the original flat icon tile when no image is set, so an
   admin who only renames a tile (no photo) keeps the old clean look instead of an empty box. */
const TILE_ICONS = {
  character: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><circle cx="12" cy="6" r="3"/><path d="M12 9v6M12 12l-5 4M12 12l5 3M12 15l-4 6M12 15l4 6"/></svg>',
  video: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M23 7l-7 5 7 5V7z"/><rect x="1" y="5" width="15" height="14" rx="2"/></svg>',
  templates: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M4 19V5a2 2 0 012-2h9l5 5v11a2 2 0 01-2 2H6a2 2 0 01-2-2z"/></svg>',
  keyframe: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="M12 7v5l3 3"/></svg>',
  tutorial: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="2" y="4" width="20" height="16" rx="3"/><path d="M10 9.5v5l4.5-2.5z" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"/></svg>',
  spark: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 2v6M12 16v6M2 12h6M16 12h6M5 5l4 4M15 15l4 4M19 5l-4 4M9 15l-4 4"/></svg>'
};
let _tilesSig = null;

/* ---- Tile image persistence ----
   BUGFIX (tile pictures vanished after a few hours / never loaded): a tile's picture was only
   ever kept as a plain imgBB URL. The cache above stores that URL, not the picture itself, so
   any slow connection, a CDN hiccup or a link that later stops working left a blank tile. Two
   things were wrong:
     1) the failure fallback was an inline onerror="…innerHTML=${JSON.stringify(svg)}" — JSON
        output is full of double quotes, which end a double-quoted HTML attribute early, so
        that handler was a syntax error and NEVER ran: a failed picture left an empty box
        instead of the icon (exactly the blank "Templates" tile);
     2) nothing retried or remembered the picture.
   Now: every tile picture is fetched once, shrunk to a small JPEG/PNG data URL and kept in
   localStorage keyed by its URL, so it keeps showing even if imgBB is slow, blocked or the
   link has expired. The live URL is still tried first (it may be newer), with one retry, and
   the icon fallback is attached with a real listener instead of an inline attribute. */
const TILE_IMG_CACHE_KEY = 'riggy.tileImg.cache.v2'; // bumped: v1 entries were cached before high-quality smoothing was added
const TILE_IMG_MAX_ENTRIES = 12;
const TILE_IMG_MAX_DIM = 320; // tiles are ~80-100 css px square; 320 keeps them sharp on 3x screens
let tileImgCache = {};
try{ tileImgCache = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(TILE_IMG_CACHE_KEY) || '{}') || {}; }catch(e){ tileImgCache = {}; }
function saveTileImgCache(){
  try{
    localStorage.setItem(TILE_IMG_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(tileImgCache));
  }catch(e){
    // Storage full: drop the oldest half and try once more, never throw into the UI.
    try{
      const keys = Object.keys(tileImgCache);
      keys.slice(0, Math.ceil(keys.length/2)).forEach(k=>delete tileImgCache[k]);
      localStorage.setItem(TILE_IMG_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(tileImgCache));
    }catch(e2){}
  }
}
// Downloads the picture, shrinks it on a canvas and stores it as a data URL. Best-effort:
// a cross-origin host that doesn't send CORS headers simply can't be snapshotted (canvas
// would be tainted) — in that case nothing is stored and the live URL keeps being used.
function cacheTileImage(url){
  if(!/^https?:/i.test(url) || tileImgCache[url] || cacheTileImage._busy[url]) return;
  cacheTileImage._busy[url] = true;
  const probe = new Image();
  probe.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
  probe.onload = ()=>{
    try{
      const scale = Math.min(1, TILE_IMG_MAX_DIM / Math.max(probe.naturalWidth, probe.naturalHeight));
      const w = Math.max(1, Math.round(probe.naturalWidth * scale));
      const h = Math.max(1, Math.round(probe.naturalHeight * scale));
      const cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = w; cv.height = h;
      const tctx = cv.getContext('2d');
      tctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;
      tctx.imageSmoothingQuality = 'high';
      tctx.drawImage(probe, 0, 0, w, h);
      // PNG keeps transparency for cut-out tile art; fall back to JPEG if it's huge.
      let data = cv.toDataURL('image/png');
      if(data.length > 90000) data = cv.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.82);
      if(data.length <= 140000){
        const keys = Object.keys(tileImgCache);
        if(keys.length >= TILE_IMG_MAX_ENTRIES) delete tileImgCache[keys[0]];
        tileImgCache[url] = data;
        saveTileImgCache();
      }
    }catch(e){ /* tainted canvas / quota — live URL still works */ }
    delete cacheTileImage._busy[url];
  };
  probe.onerror = ()=>{ delete cacheTileImage._busy[url]; };
  probe.src = url;
}
cacheTileImage._busy = {};

function tileShowFallback(thumb, iconKey){
  if(!thumb) return;
  thumb.classList.remove('tile-photo');
  thumb.innerHTML = TILE_ICONS[iconKey] || TILE_ICONS.spark;
}
// Wires load/error handling onto one tile <img>. Order tried on failure:
//   live URL -> live URL once more (cache-busted) -> locally saved copy -> icon fallback.
function wireTileImage(img, url, iconKey){
  const thumb = img.closest('.home-tile-thumb');
  let stage = 0;
  img.addEventListener('load', ()=>{
    img.classList.add('loaded');
    if(img.src === url || img.dataset.live === '1') cacheTileImage(url);
  });
  img.addEventListener('error', ()=>{
    stage++;
    if(stage === 1 && /^https?:/i.test(url)){
      setTimeout(()=>{ img.dataset.live = '1'; img.src = url + (url.indexOf('?') > -1 ? '&' : '?') + 'r=' + Date.now(); }, 1500);
    } else if(stage <= 2 && tileImgCache[url]){
      stage = 3; img.dataset.live = '0'; img.src = tileImgCache[url];
    } else {
      tileShowFallback(thumb, iconKey);
    }
  });
  img.dataset.live = '1';
  // Paint the saved copy instantly when we have one (no blank flash on a cold/slow network),
  // then let the live URL replace it if it loads.
  if(tileImgCache[url]){
    img.src = tileImgCache[url];
    img.dataset.live = '0';
    const live = new Image();
    live.onload = ()=>{ img.dataset.live = '1'; img.src = url; };
    live.onerror = ()=>{ /* keep the saved copy showing */ };
    if(/^https?:/i.test(url)) live.src = url;
  } else {
    img.src = url;
  }
  // A picture that already finished (or failed) before the listeners existed would otherwise
  // sit invisible — settle it explicitly.
  if(img.complete){
    if(img.naturalWidth > 0) img.classList.add('loaded');
    else img.dispatchEvent(new Event('error'));
  }
}
function renderHomeTiles(){
  const wrap = document.getElementById('homeTileRow');
  if(!wrap) return;
  const tiles = getTiles();
  const sig = JSON.stringify(tiles);
  if(sig === _tilesSig) return;
  _tilesSig = sig;
  wrap.innerHTML = tiles.map((t, i)=>{
    const img = safeImageUrl(t.image);
    const isPromptTile = String(t.actionValue||'') === 'keyframeInfo' || String(t.label||'').trim().toLowerCase() === 'keyframe';
    const tileLabel = isPromptTile ? 'Prompts' : (t.label||'');
    const tileIcon = isPromptTile ? 'templates' : (t.icon||'spark');
    const attrs = ` data-atype="${escapeHtml(t.actionType||'ratio')}" data-avalue="${escapeHtml(t.actionValue||'')}"`;
    const thumb = img
      ? `<div class="home-tile-thumb tile-photo" data-tile-i="${i}"><div class="home-tile-photo-card">
           <img alt="${escapeHtml(tileLabel)}" decoding="async">
         </div></div>`
      : `<div class="home-tile-thumb">${TILE_ICONS[tileIcon] || TILE_ICONS.spark}</div>`;
    return `<div class="home-tile"${attrs}>${thumb}<span>${escapeHtml(tileLabel)}</span></div>`;
  }).join('');
  // Attach picture handling AFTER the markup exists (no inline handlers, so no quoting traps).
  tiles.forEach((t, i)=>{
    const img = safeImageUrl(t.image);
    if(!img) return;
    const el = wrap.querySelector('.home-tile-thumb[data-tile-i="'+i+'"] img');
    if(el) wireTileImage(el, img, t.icon);
  });
}

/* ---------------- RENDER: EXAMPLE ANIMATIONS ---------------- */
function renderHomeAnimations(){
  const wrap = document.getElementById('homeVideoScroll');
  if(!wrap) return;
  const anims = getAnimations();
  const sig = JSON.stringify(anims);
  if(sig === _animSig) return;
  _animSig = sig;

  const stage = '<div class="home-video-stage"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#4E7CFF" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><circle cx="12" cy="6" r="3"/><path d="M12 9v6M12 12l-5 4M12 12l5 3M12 15l-4 6M12 15l4 6"/></svg></div>';
  const aspectMap = { '9:16':'9 / 16', '16:9':'16 / 9', '1:1':'1 / 1', '4:3':'4 / 3', '9/16':'9 / 16', '16/9':'16 / 9', '1/1':'1 / 1', '4/3':'4 / 3' };
  wrap.innerHTML = anims.map(a=>{
    const img = safeImageUrl(a.image);
    const video = (typeof (a.video || a.videoUrl) === 'string' && /^https?:\/\//i.test(a.video || a.videoUrl)) ? (a.video || a.videoUrl) : '';
    const aspect = aspectMap[a.aspectRatio] || '9 / 16';
    const media = video
      ? `<video class="home-video-img home-video-preview" src="${escapeHtml(video)}" muted autoplay loop playsinline preload="metadata" aria-label="${escapeHtml(a.label||'Example animation')}" onerror="this.style.display='none'"></video>`
      : (img ? `<img class="home-video-img" src="${escapeHtml(img)}" alt="${escapeHtml(a.label||'')}" decoding="async" loading="lazy" onerror="this.style.display='none'">` : '');
    const thumb = media ? `${stage}${media}` : stage;
    return `
      <div class="home-video-card" style="aspect-ratio:${aspect};" data-atype="${escapeHtml(a.actionType||'screen')}" data-avalue="${escapeHtml(a.actionValue||'')}">
        <div class="home-video-glow"></div>
        ${thumb}
        <div class="home-video-play"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M8 5v14l11-7z"/></svg></div>
        <div class="home-video-label">${escapeHtml(a.label||'')}</div>
      </div>`;
  }).join('');
}

/* ---------------- RENDER: TUTORIALS (YouTube-style grid) ----------------
   Admin-managed via the admin panel's Tutorials page (title + video link + thumbnail), fed
   through the same siteContent/main Firestore doc as banners/tiles/animations. Each card
   opens its video link in a new tab via the same runAdminAction('link', …) every other piece
   of admin content already uses — no separate player needed. */
const TUTORIAL_PLACEHOLDER_SVG = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#4E7CFF" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="2" y="4" width="20" height="16" rx="3"/><path d="M10 9.5v5l4.5-2.5z" fill="#4E7CFF" stroke="none"/></svg>';
let tutorialSearchQuery = '';
// Typing re-filters the already-loaded list client-side (no extra Firestore reads) — fine at
// this scale, and keeps results instant as you type, like YouTube's own search-as-you-type feel.
function onTutorialSearchInput(value){
  tutorialSearchQuery = (value || '').trim();
  const bar = document.getElementById('tutorialSearchBar');
  if(bar) bar.classList.toggle('has-query', tutorialSearchQuery.length > 0);
  _tutorialsSig = null; // force a re-render even though the underlying data hasn't changed
  renderTutorialsGrid();
}
function clearTutorialSearch(){
  const input = document.getElementById('tutorialSearchInput');
  if(input) input.value = '';
  onTutorialSearchInput('');
  if(input) input.focus();
}
function renderTutorialsGrid(){
  const wrap = document.getElementById('tutorialGrid');
  if(!wrap) return;
  const allTutorials = getTutorials();
  const q = tutorialSearchQuery.toLowerCase();
  const tutorials = q ? allTutorials.filter(t=>(t.title||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)) : allTutorials;
  const sig = JSON.stringify(tutorials) + '|' + q;
  if(sig === _tutorialsSig) return;
  _tutorialsSig = sig;
  if(!allTutorials.length){
    wrap.innerHTML = `<div class="tutorial-empty">No tutorials yet — check back soon.</div>`;
    return;
  }
  if(!tutorials.length){
    wrap.innerHTML = `<div class="tutorial-empty">No tutorials match “${escapeHtml(tutorialSearchQuery)}”.</div>`;
    return;
  }
  wrap.innerHTML = tutorials.map(t=>{
    const img = safeImageUrl(t.image);
    const thumb = img
      ? `<img src="${escapeHtml(img)}" alt="${escapeHtml(t.title||'')}" decoding="async" loading="lazy" onerror="this.closest('.tutorial-thumb-wrap').classList.add('failed'); this.style.display='none';">`
      : `<div class="tutorial-thumb-fallback">${TUTORIAL_PLACEHOLDER_SVG}</div>`;
    return `
      <div class="tutorial-card" data-atype="link" data-avalue="${escapeHtml(t.videoUrl||'')}">
        <div class="tutorial-thumb-wrap">
          ${thumb}
          <div class="tutorial-play-badge"><svg viewBox="0 0 68 48"><path d="M66.5 7.7c-.8-2.9-2.6-5.1-5.3-5.8C56.5 0.5 34 0.5 34 0.5s-22.5 0-27.2 1.4C4.1 2.6 2.3 4.8 1.5 7.7 0.1 12.5 0.1 24 0.1 24s0 11.5 1.4 16.3c.8 2.9 2.6 5.1 5.3 5.8C11.5 47.5 34 47.5 34 47.5s22.5 0 27.2-1.4c2.7-.7 4.5-2.9 5.3-5.8C67.9 35.5 67.9 24 67.9 24s0-11.5-1.4-16.3z" fill="#FF0000" opacity="0.92"/><path d="M27 34l18-10-18-10v20z" fill="#fff"/></svg></div>
        </div>
        <div class="tutorial-meta">
          <div class="tutorial-meta-icon"><img src="https://i.postimg.cc/HkD7s6pT/file-00000000c3a88211816ac81485d9da1b.png" alt="Tutorials" loading="lazy" decoding="async"></div>
          <div>
            <p class="tutorial-title">${escapeHtml(t.title||'Untitled')}</p>
            <p class="tutorial-sub">Tutorial · Ethvelle</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>`;
  }).join('');
}

/* ---------------- RENDER: PAYMENT PLANS ---------------- */
/* ================= PLAN PRICING (admin discount + first-purchase offer + promo popup) =====
   A configured promo-popup discount has priority over plan-level and first-purchase discounts
   for its target plan. A popup may target one plan or all paid plans (__all_paid__). If no promo
   applies to a plan, the best applicable regular discount is used as before.
   Returns { finalPrice, originalPrice, off, label } or null if no discount applies. */
function computePlanDiscount(plan){
  const price = Number(plan.price) || 0;
  if(price <= 0) return null;
  function offFor(d){
    if(!d || !d.on) return 0;
    const v = Number(d.value) || 0;
    return d.type === 'amount' ? v : Math.round(price * v / 100);
  }
  const pd = window.activePromoDiscount;
  const promoTargetsPlan = pd && (pd.applyToAll === true || pd.planId === '__all_paid__' || pd.planId === plan.id);
  if(promoTargetsPlan){
    const value = Number(pd.value) || 0;
    const promoOff = pd.type === 'amount' ? value : Math.round(price * value / 100);
    if(promoOff > 0){
      const off = Math.min(promoOff, price); // never discount below ৳0
      return { finalPrice: price - off, originalPrice: price, off, label: pd.label || 'Promo discount', isPromo:true };
    }
  }
  const candidates = [];
  if(plan.discount && plan.discount.on){
    const off = offFor(plan.discount);
    if(off > 0) candidates.push({ off, label: plan.discount.label || 'Discount' });
  }
  if(plan.firstPurchaseOffer && plan.firstPurchaseOffer.on && currentUser && !myHasEverOrdered){
    const off = offFor(plan.firstPurchaseOffer);
    if(off > 0) candidates.push({ off, label: plan.firstPurchaseOffer.label || 'First purchase offer' });
  }
  if(!candidates.length) return null;
  candidates.sort((a,b)=>b.off - a.off);
  const best = candidates[0];
  const off = Math.min(best.off, price); // never discount below ৳0
  return { finalPrice: price - off, originalPrice: price, off, label: best.label };
}
function renderPaymentPlans(){
  const scroll = document.querySelector('#screen-payment .demo-scroll');
  if(!scroll) return;
  const plans = getPlans();
  // Whether the signed-in user currently holds ANY paid (non-free) plan — once true, the
  // Free card is no longer shown at all, the same way most subscription apps stop offering
  // you the free tier once you're already a paying customer. Signed-out visitors and
  // Free-tier users still see every card, Free included.
  const hasActivePaidPlan = !!(currentUser && myActivePlanId && myActivePlanId !== 'free');
  const orderSig = myLatestOrderInfo ? [myLatestOrderInfo.id,myLatestOrderInfo.planId,myLatestOrderInfo.status].join(':') : '';
  const planOrderSig = JSON.stringify(Object.keys(myPlanOrderStates).sort().map(k=>[k,myPlanOrderStates[k].id,myPlanOrderStates[k].status]));
  const sig = JSON.stringify(plans) + '|' + myActivePlanId + '|' + (currentUser?'in':'out') + '|' + myHasEverOrdered + '|' + orderSig + '|' + planOrderSig + '|' + JSON.stringify(window.activePromoDiscount||null);
  if(sig === _plansSig) return;
  _plansSig = sig;
  // The user's own plan always sorts to the very top of the list, ahead of every other
  // card, so they see "this is what I have" first rather than having to scroll to find it.
  const ordered = plans.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
    const aMine = currentUser && a.id === myActivePlanId;
    const bMine = currentUser && b.id === myActivePlanId;
    if(aMine && !bMine) return -1;
    if(bMine && !aMine) return 1;
    return 0;
  });
  const cardsHtml = ordered.filter(p=>{
    const isFree = Number(p.price) === 0;
    // Drop the Free card once the user already has a paid plan active.
    if(isFree && hasActivePaidPlan) return false;
    return true;
  }).map(p=>{
    const isFree = Number(p.price) === 0;
    const disc = computePlanDiscount(p);
    // priceHtml shows the struck-through original price next to the discounted one when a
    // discount applies; otherwise just the plain price, same as before.
    const priceHtml = isFree
      ? '৳0<span>/month</span>'
      : disc
        ? `<span class="plan-price-strike">৳${disc.originalPrice}</span> ৳${disc.finalPrice}<span>/month</span>`
        : `৳${p.price}<span>/month</span>`;
    const featuresHtml = (p.features||[]).map(f=>`<div class="plan-feature"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M20 6L9 17l-5-5"/></svg>${escapeHtml(f)}</div>`).join('');
    const planCreditGrant = getPlanCreditGrant(p);
    const creditsHtml = planCreditGrant > 0 ? `<div class="plan-feature"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M12 2v20M17 5H9.5a3.5 3.5 0 000 7h5a3.5 3.5 0 010 7H6"/></svg>${planCreditGrant.toLocaleString()} credits per approved payment</div>` : '';
    // A card is the user's current plan if they're signed in and its id matches their
    // admin-approved plan (myActivePlanId), OR it's the free card and they have no active
    // paid plan at all. Everything else always reads "Upgrade to X", even the ৳0 card, so a
    // Pro user browsing back to Free correctly sees "Upgrade" isn't offered but Free isn't
    // mislabeled as their current plan either.
    const isMine = currentUser ? (p.id === myActivePlanId) : isFree;
    // A submitted pending order replaces the Upgrade button on that exact plan card.
    // Normalize ids/status because admin-published plan ids may not use the built-in names.
    const orderForPlan = currentUser ? (myPlanOrderStates[String(p.id)] || (myLatestOrderInfo && String(myLatestOrderInfo.planId) === String(p.id) ? myLatestOrderInfo : null)) : null;
    const orderStatusForPlan = String(orderForPlan && orderForPlan.status || '').toLowerCase();
    const hasPendingOrder = !!orderForPlan && orderStatusForPlan === 'pending';
    const ctaHtml = (isMine || hasPendingOrder) ? '' : `<button class="plan-cta" onclick="event.stopPropagation(); confirmPlan('${p.id}')">Upgrade to ${escapeHtml(p.name)}</button>`;
    // Badge: "Current" always wins (shows what the user actually owns). Otherwise, a discount
    // badge takes priority over the plain custom badge, since "33% off" is more useful to see
    // at a glance than "Popular" — but a plan can still carry both a badgeText AND a discount;
    // only one badge chip is shown at a time to avoid crowding the card.
    let badgeHtml = '';
    if(isMine){
      badgeHtml = '<div class="plan-badge plan-badge-current">Current</div>';
    } else if(orderForPlan && orderStatusForPlan === 'pending'){
      badgeHtml = '<div class="plan-badge plan-badge-order pending">Order pending</div>';
    } else if(orderForPlan && orderStatusForPlan === 'rejected'){
      badgeHtml = '<div class="plan-badge plan-badge-order rejected">Order rejected</div>';
    } else if(orderForPlan && orderStatusForPlan === 'approved'){
      badgeHtml = '<div class="plan-badge plan-badge-order approved">Order approved</div>';
    } else if(disc){
      const pct = Math.round(disc.off / disc.originalPrice * 100);
      badgeHtml = disc.isPromo
        ? `<div class="plan-badge plan-badge-discount"><span class="promo-discount-p" title="Promo discount">P</span>${pct}% off</div>`
        : `<div class="plan-badge plan-badge-discount">${escapeHtml(disc.label)} \u2014 ${pct}% off</div>`;
    } else if(p.popular && p.badgeText){
      badgeHtml = `<div class="plan-badge">${escapeHtml(p.badgeText)}</div>`;
    }
    return `
      <div class="plan-card${p.popular?' featured':''}${isMine?' current-plan':''}" data-plan="${p.id}" data-name="${escapeHtml(p.name)}" onclick="selectPlan(this, true)">
        <div class="plan-card-top">
          ${badgeHtml}
          <div class="plan-radio"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M20 6L9 17l-5-5"/></svg></div>
        </div>
        <div class="plan-name">${escapeHtml(p.name)}</div>
        <p class="plan-price">${priceHtml}</p>
        <p class="plan-desc">${escapeHtml(p.desc||'')}</p>
        <div class="plan-feature-list">${featuresHtml}${creditsHtml}</div>
        ${ctaHtml}
      </div>`;
  }).join('');
  scroll.innerHTML = cardsHtml;
  // No plan is highlighted merely because it is the current/default plan.
  // A border and checked radio appear only after the visitor explicitly taps a card.
  scroll.querySelectorAll('.plan-card.selected').forEach(card=>card.classList.remove('selected'));
  selectedPlan = null;
}

// Populate the Home screen and Payment screen once immediately (from cache/defaults so
// there's never a blank flash), then again as soon as a live Firestore read comes back.
wireContentTaps();
(function wireCreateSectionTracking(){
  const home = document.getElementById('screen-home');
  if(!home || home.dataset.createTrackingWired) return;
  home.dataset.createTrackingWired = '1';
  home.addEventListener('click', function(event){
    const tile = event.target && event.target.closest ? event.target.closest('.home-tile') : null;
    const createButton = event.target && event.target.closest ? event.target.closest('.home-nav-create-btn') : null;
    if(!tile && !createButton) return;
    const labelEl = tile ? tile.querySelector('span') : null;
    trackEvent('create_section_click', {
      source:tile ? 'what_would_you_create_tile' : 'home_create_button',
      itemLabel:labelEl ? labelEl.textContent.trim().slice(0,70) : 'Create button',
      screen:'home'
    });
  });
})();

if(firebaseReady && fbDb){
  fbDb.collection('siteContent').doc('main').onSnapshot({ includeMetadataChanges:true }, function(doc){
    const fromCache=!!(doc.metadata&&doc.metadata.fromCache);
    if(doc.exists){
      const data=doc.data()||{};
      siteContent={
        banners:data.banners||null,animations:data.animations||null,plans:data.plans||null,tiles:data.tiles||null,
        tutorials:data.tutorials||null,contactInfo:data.contactInfo||null,
        creditSettings:data.creditSettings||siteContent.creditSettings||defaultCreditSettings(),
        promoPopups:(splitSiteContentLoaded.promoPopups&&splitSiteContentExists.promoPopups)?(siteContent.promoPopups||[]):(data.promoPopups||null),
        customAnimPresets:data.customAnimPresets||null,
        prompts:(splitSiteContentLoaded.prompts&&splitSiteContentExists.prompts)?(siteContent.prompts||[]):(data.prompts||[]),
        assets:(splitSiteContentLoaded.assets&&splitSiteContentExists.assets)?(siteContent.assets||[]):(data.assets||[]),
        models:(splitSiteContentLoaded.models&&splitSiteContentExists.models)?(siteContent.models||[]):(data.models||[]),
        animationAccess:(splitSiteContentLoaded.animationAccess&&splitSiteContentExists.animationAccess)?(siteContent.animationAccess||{}):(data.animationAccess||{})
      };
      publishedModelAssets = Array.isArray(siteContent.models) ? siteContent.models.map(m=>({...m,origin:'admin'})) : [];
      try{localStorage.setItem(SITE_CONTENT_CACHE_KEY,JSON.stringify(siteContent));}catch(e){}
      if(typeof renderModelsTab==='function' && document.getElementById('modelsGrid')) renderModelsTab();
      if(typeof renderModelLibraryList==='function' && document.getElementById('modelAssetListBody')) renderModelLibraryList();
      renderHomeTiles();renderHomeBanners();renderHomeAnimations();renderPaymentPlans();renderTutorialsGrid();renderPromptLibrary();applyCustomAnimPresets();

      if(typeof renderAnimList==='function'&&document.getElementById('animListBody'))renderAnimList();
      if(document.getElementById('editorAssetLibrary'))renderAssetsPanel();
      if(document.getElementById('screen-help')&&document.getElementById('screen-help').classList.contains('active'))renderHelpContactCards();
    }
    if(!fromCache){promoPopupContentServerConfirmed=true;wirePromoPopups(true);}
    else wirePromoPopups();
  },function(err){
    console.warn('Ethvelle: could not read siteContent from Firestore, using cached/default content',err);
    promoPopupContentServerConfirmed=true;wirePromoPopups(true);
  });
  // New, smaller documents are authoritative for prompts, assets, popup targeting, and animation access.
  // If a split document does not exist yet, the legacy value read from siteContent/main is retained.
  SPLIT_SITE_CONTENT_KEYS.forEach(key=>{
    const unsub=fbDb.collection('siteContent').doc(key).onSnapshot({includeMetadataChanges:true},function(doc){
      splitSiteContentLoaded[key]=true; splitSiteContentExists[key]=!!doc.exists;
      if(doc.exists){
        const data=doc.data()||{};
        if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(data,key))siteContent[key]=data[key];
      }
      try{localStorage.setItem(SITE_CONTENT_CACHE_KEY,JSON.stringify(siteContent));}catch(e){}
      if(key==='prompts')renderPromptLibrary();
      if(key==='assets'&&document.getElementById('editorAssetLibrary'))renderAssetsPanel();
      if(key==='models'){publishedModelAssets=Array.isArray(siteContent.models)?siteContent.models.map(m=>({...m,origin:'admin'})):[];if(typeof renderModelsTab==='function')renderModelsTab();if(typeof renderModelLibraryList==='function')renderModelLibraryList();}
      if(key==='animationAccess'&&typeof renderAnimList==='function'&&document.getElementById('animListBody'))renderAnimList();
      if(key==='promoPopups'){
        if(doc.metadata&&!doc.metadata.fromCache){promoPopupContentServerConfirmed=true;wirePromoPopups(true);}
        else wirePromoPopups();
      }
    },function(err){console.warn('Ethvelle: could not read siteContent/'+key,err);});
    splitSiteContentUnsubs.push(unsub);
  });
}

<script>
/* ============================================================================
   ETHVELLE CHARACTER QUALITY UPDATE
   - Optional Gemini landmark detection (body, face, palm, thumb and 5 fingers)
   - Blink / talk facial overlays
   - Hand-pose clips, plus a hardened mesh deformation path above
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   SECURITY: this HTML placeholder is client-side. For a public production site,
   proxy Gemini requests through your server and keep the API key there.
   ============================================================================ */
window.ETHVELLE_GEMINI_CONFIG = window.ETHVELLE_GEMINI_CONFIG || {
  apiKey: 'PASTE_YOUR_GEMINI_API_KEY_HERE', // <-- add your Google AI Studio API key here for local testing
  model: 'gemini-3.5-flash'                // requested model ID; currently auto-routed by Google to 3.6 Flash
};
var faceAnimationTimeOverride = null;

function ethvelleHasGeminiKey(){
  const k=String(window.ETHVELLE_GEMINI_CONFIG?.apiKey||'').trim();
  return !!k && k!=='PASTE_YOUR_GEMINI_API_KEY_HERE' && k!=='YOUR_GEMINI_API_KEY';
}
function ethvelleSafePoint(p){
  if(!p || typeof p!=='object') return null;
  const x=Number(p.x), y=Number(p.y);
  if(!Number.isFinite(x)||!Number.isFinite(y)||x<0||x>1||y<0||y>1) return null;
  return {x,y};
}
function ethvelleCopyPoints(src){
  const out={};
  Object.keys(src||{}).forEach(k=>{const p=ethvelleSafePoint(src[k]);if(p)out[k]=p;});
  return out;
}
function ethvelleEstimateFace(srcCanvas,joints){
  if(!srcCanvas||!joints||!joints.headTop||!joints.neck) return null;
  const top=joints.headTop, neck=joints.neck;
  const headH=Math.max(0.025,neck.y-top.y);
  const shoulderSpan=Math.abs((joints.shoulderR?.x??(top.x+0.12))-(joints.shoulderL?.x??(top.x-0.12)));
  const headW=Math.max(0.045,Math.min(0.34,shoulderSpan*0.65||headH*0.75));
  const cx=neck.x;
  const face={
    leftEye:{x:Math.max(0.005,cx-headW*0.19),y:Math.min(.99,top.y+headH*.49),w:headW*.18,h:headH*.075},
    rightEye:{x:Math.min(.995,cx+headW*0.19),y:Math.min(.99,top.y+headH*.49),w:headW*.18,h:headH*.075},
    mouth:{x:cx,y:Math.min(.995,top.y+headH*.88),w:headW*.24,h:headH*.095},
    source:'estimated'
  };
  try{
    const ctx=srcCanvas.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
    const px=Math.round(Math.max(0,Math.min(srcCanvas.width-1,cx*srcCanvas.width)));
    const py=Math.round(Math.max(0,Math.min(srcCanvas.height-1,(top.y+headH*.66)*srcCanvas.height)));
    const r=Math.max(2,Math.round(Math.min(srcCanvas.width,srcCanvas.height)*.006));
    const d=ctx.getImageData(Math.max(0,px-r),Math.max(0,py-r),Math.min(srcCanvas.width-px+r,r*2+1),Math.min(srcCanvas.height-py+r,r*2+1)).data;
    let R=0,G=0,B=0,n=0;
    for(let i=0;i<d.length;i+=4){
      if(d[i+3]<120)continue;
      const lum=.2126*d[i]+.7152*d[i+1]+.0722*d[i+2];
      if(lum<38||lum>246)continue;
      R+=d[i];G+=d[i+1];B+=d[i+2];n++;
    }
    if(n) face.skinColor='#'+[R/n,G/n,B/n].map(v=>Math.round(v).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
  }catch(e){}
  face.skinColor=face.skinColor||'#dba982';
  return face;
}
function ethvelleNormalizeFace(raw,srcCanvas,joints){
  const fallback=ethvelleEstimateFace(srcCanvas,joints);
  if(!raw||typeof raw!=='object') return fallback;
  const out={...(fallback||{}),source:'gemini'};
  ['leftEye','rightEye','mouth'].forEach(k=>{
    const p=raw[k];
    const pos=ethvelleSafePoint(p);
    if(!pos)return;
    const w=Number(p.w),h=Number(p.h);
    if(!Number.isFinite(w)||!Number.isFinite(h)||w<=0||h<=0)return;
    out[k]={x:pos.x,y:pos.y,w:Math.min(.4,w),h:Math.min(.3,h)};
  });
  if(/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(String(raw.skinColor||'')))out.skinColor=raw.skinColor;
  return out;
}
async function analyzeAutoRigWithGemini(silent){
  if(!autoRigState||!autoRigState.srcCanvas||!autoRigState.joints){showToast('Upload a character first',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:1800});return;}
  if(!ethvelleHasGeminiKey()){
    const status=document.getElementById('autoRigGeminiStatus');
    if(status)status.textContent='Add your API key to ETHVELLE_GEMINI_CONFIG.apiKey near the end of this file, then try again.';
    if(!silent)showToast('Gemini key missing — see ETHVELLE_GEMINI_CONFIG at the end of the HTML file.',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:3000});
    return;
  }
  const btn=document.getElementById('autoRigGeminiBtn'),status=document.getElementById('autoRigGeminiStatus');
  if(btn){btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent='Analyzing body, fingers and face…';}
  if(status)status.textContent='Gemini is estimating normalized joint positions. You can still correct points afterward.';
  try{
    const dataUrl=autoRigState.srcCanvas.toDataURL('image/png');
    const m=dataUrl.match(/^data:(image\/[a-zA-Z0-9.+-]+);base64,(.+)$/);
    if(!m)throw new Error('Could not encode image for Gemini.');
    const prompt=`Analyze this single full-body 2D cartoon character image for a 2D puppet rig. Return ONLY JSON. Coordinates x,y are normalized to the whole image from 0 to 1, origin top-left. Use image-left as L and image-right as R to match the editor. Body joints needed: headTop (top of skull), neck, shoulderL, shoulderR (shoulder anchors close to the torso centerline), elbowL, wristL, elbowR, wristR, hip center, kneeL, ankleL, kneeR, ankleR. For each visible hand, estimate palmL/palmR and thumbBase/Mid/Tip, indexBase/Mid/Tip, middleBase/Mid/Tip, ringBase/Mid/Tip, pinkyBase/Mid/Tip, with the side suffix L or R (e.g. indexBaseL). Omit or set null any point hidden or too small to locate confidently; do not invent precise tiny fingertips if they are not visible. Also return face leftEye, rightEye and mouth objects as {x,y,w,h}, where x,y are the feature center and w,h are normalized feature width and height. Estimate skinColor as a 6-digit hex color near the eyes/mouth, not the hair or clothes. Keep joints on the actual drawn anatomy, avoid background pixels and keep headTop above neck, neck above hip, and ankles below knees. JSON shape: {"joints":{"headTop":{"x":0.5,"y":0.1},...},"face":{"leftEye":{"x":0.46,"y":0.18,"w":0.03,"h":0.01},"rightEye":...,"mouth":...,"skinColor":"#dba982"},"confidence":0.8,"warnings":[]}.`;
    const url='https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/'+encodeURIComponent(window.ETHVELLE_GEMINI_CONFIG.model||'gemini-3.5-flash')+':generateContent?key='+encodeURIComponent(window.ETHVELLE_GEMINI_CONFIG.apiKey.trim());
    const res=await fetch(url,{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({contents:[{parts:[{text:prompt},{inline_data:{mime_type:m[1],data:m[2]}}]}],generationConfig:{temperature:0.1,responseMimeType:'application/json'}})});
    const payload=await res.json().catch(()=>({}));
    if(!res.ok)throw new Error(payload?.error?.message||('Gemini request failed ('+res.status+').'));
    const textOut=(payload.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts||[]).map(p=>p.text||'').join('').trim();
    const jsonText=textOut.replace(/^```(?:json)?\s*/i,'').replace(/\s*```$/,'');
    const parsed=JSON.parse(jsonText);
    const detected=ethvelleCopyPoints(parsed.joints||{});
    const required=['headTop','neck','shoulderL','shoulderR','elbowL','elbowR','wristL','wristR','hip','kneeL','kneeR','ankleL','ankleR'];
    const merged={...autoRigState.joints};
    required.forEach(k=>{if(detected[k])merged[k]=detected[k];});
    // Keep Gemini's lateral shoulder positions; buildRigOverrideFromJoints converts them to
    // torso-local attachment offsets so the shoulder pivots stay on the drawn silhouette.
    const validOrder=merged.headTop.y<merged.neck.y&&merged.neck.y<merged.hip.y&&merged.kneeL.y>merged.hip.y&&merged.kneeR.y>merged.hip.y&&merged.ankleL.y>merged.kneeL.y&&merged.ankleR.y>merged.kneeR.y;
    if(!validOrder)throw new Error('Gemini returned inconsistent body-joint order. Use the local rig and adjust the dots manually.');
    Object.keys(detected).forEach(k=>{if(!required.includes(k))merged[k]=detected[k];});
    autoRigState.joints=merged;
    autoRigState.originalJoints=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(merged));
    autoRigState.attachAt={armL:Math.max(0.2,Math.min(0.98,(merged.hip.y-merged.shoulderL.y)/Math.max(.01,merged.hip.y-merged.neck.y))),armR:Math.max(0.2,Math.min(.98,(merged.hip.y-merged.shoulderR.y)/Math.max(.01,merged.hip.y-merged.neck.y)))};
    autoRigState.face=ethvelleNormalizeFace(parsed.face,autoRigState.srcCanvas,merged);
    autoRigState.aiProvider='Gemini';
    autoRigState.aiConfidence=Number(parsed.confidence)||null;
    autoRigState.handRig=!!(merged.palmL&&merged.palmR&&['L','R'].some(side=>['thumb','index','middle','ring','pinky'].some(f=>merged[f+'Base'+side]&&merged[f+'Mid'+side]&&merged[f+'Tip'+side])));
    autoRigState.warnings=Array.isArray(parsed.warnings)?parsed.warnings.filter(x=>typeof x==='string').slice(0,5):[];
    renderAutoRigWarnings();renderAutoRigOverlay();
    if(status)status.textContent='Gemini analysis complete. Check the points before creating the rig; missing fingertips may still need manual correction.';
    if(!silent)showToast('Gemini detected the body and face landmarks',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1800});
  }catch(err){
    console.warn('Ethvelle Gemini rig analysis failed:',err);
    if(status)status.textContent='Gemini analysis failed: '+(err.message||'unknown error')+' Local detection is still available.';
    if(!silent)showToast('Gemini analysis failed — local rig is unchanged.',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2600});
  }finally{
    if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent='Analyze with Gemini · hands + face';}
  }
}

// Gemini finger points are also draggable in the upload preview; small gold dots distinguish them.
(function installDetailedRigOverlay(){
  if(typeof renderAutoRigOverlay!=='function')return;
  const original=renderAutoRigOverlay;
  renderAutoRigOverlay=function(){
    original();
    if(!autoRigState||!autoRigState.joints)return;
    const layer=document.getElementById('autoRigJointLayer');
    if(!layer)return;
    const existingKeys=new Set(AUTORIG_JOINT_KEYS);
    const extra=Object.keys(autoRigState.joints).filter(k=>!existingKeys.has(k)&&/^(palm[LR]|(thumb|index|middle|ring|pinky)(Base|Mid|Tip)[LR])$/.test(k));
    const pairs=[];
    ['L','R'].forEach(side=>{
      if(autoRigState.joints['wrist'+side]&&autoRigState.joints['palm'+side])pairs.push(['wrist'+side,'palm'+side]);
      ['thumb','index','middle','ring','pinky'].forEach(f=>{
        const a=f+'Base'+side,b=f+'Mid'+side,c=f+'Tip'+side;
        if(autoRigState.joints[a]&&autoRigState.joints[b])pairs.push([a,b]);
        if(autoRigState.joints[b]&&autoRigState.joints[c])pairs.push([b,c]);
      });
    });
    if(!layer.__evv109FingerBoneNodes)layer.__evv109FingerBoneNodes=[];
    if(!layer.__evv109FingerDotNodes)layer.__evv109FingerDotNodes=new Map();
    const boneNodes=layer.__evv109FingerBoneNodes;
    const dotNodes=layer.__evv109FingerDotNodes;
    // Paint existing finger bones in place; no remove/insert churn during a drag.
    pairs.forEach(([a,b],index)=>{
      const pa=autoRigState.joints[a],pb=autoRigState.joints[b];if(!pa||!pb)return;
      const p0=autoRigImgFracToPx(pa.x,pa.y),p1=autoRigImgFracToPx(pb.x,pb.y),dx=p1.x-p0.x,dy=p1.y-p0.y;
      let el=boneNodes[index];
      if(!el){el=document.createElement('div');el.className='autorig-bone-line-el';el.dataset.extraBone='1';el.style.background='#F5B942';el.style.height='2px';layer.appendChild(el);boneNodes[index]=el;}
      el.style.display='block';el.style.left=p0.x+'px';el.style.top=p0.y+'px';el.style.width=Math.hypot(dx,dy)+'px';
      el.style.transform='translateY(-50%) rotate('+Math.atan2(dy,dx)+'rad)';
    });
    for(let i=pairs.length;i<boneNodes.length;i++)boneNodes[i].style.display='none';
    const active=new Set(extra);
    extra.forEach(key=>{
      const pt=autoRigState.joints[key],p=autoRigImgFracToPx(pt.x,pt.y);
      let dot=dotNodes.get(key);
      if(!dot||!dot.isConnected){dot=document.createElement('div');dot.className='autorig-joint-dot finger-joint';dot.dataset.joint=key;setupAutoRigJointDrag(dot,key);layer.appendChild(dot);dotNodes.set(key,dot);}
      dot.style.display='block';dot.style.left=p.x+'px';dot.style.top=p.y+'px';
    });
    dotNodes.forEach((dot,key)=>{if(!active.has(key))dot.style.display='none';});
  };
})();

// Save face/finger metadata alongside the original image. This wrapper keeps existing save/load behavior intact.
(function preserveAIDataOnFinalize(){
  if(typeof finalizeAutoRiggedCharacter!=='function')return;
  const original=finalizeAutoRiggedCharacter;
  finalizeAutoRiggedCharacter=function(){
    if(autoRigState&&autoRigState.srcCanvas&&autoRigState.joints){
      autoRigState.face=ethvelleNormalizeFace(autoRigState.face,autoRigState.srcCanvas,autoRigState.joints);
      autoRigState.handRig=!!(autoRigState.joints.palmL&&autoRigState.joints.palmR&&['L','R'].some(side=>['thumb','index','middle','ring','pinky'].some(f=>autoRigState.joints[f+'Base'+side]&&autoRigState.joints[f+'Mid'+side]&&autoRigState.joints[f+'Tip'+side])));
    }
    original();
  };
})();

function ethvelleFaceTime(){
  if(Number.isFinite(faceAnimationTimeOverride))return faceAnimationTimeOverride;
  return Number.isFinite(window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec)?window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec:(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec==='number'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:0);
}
function ethvelleMapFacePoint(char,point,bStart,bEnd){
  const mesh=char.skinMesh,face=char.autoRig?.face;
  if(!mesh||!mesh.restBones?.head||!point)return null;
  const rest=mesh.restBones.head,s=bStart('head'),e=bEnd('head');
  const angle=Math.atan2(e.y-s.y,e.x-s.x),len=Math.max(1e-5,Math.hypot(e.x-s.x,e.y-s.y));
  const x=point.x*mesh.srcW,y=point.y*mesh.srcH,dx=x-rest.ax,dy=y-rest.ay;
  const ca=Math.cos(-rest.angle),sa=Math.sin(-rest.angle),lx=dx*ca-dy*sa,ly=dx*sa+dy*ca;
  const scale=Math.max(.92,Math.min(1.08,len/rest.len)),rot=Math.cos(angle),sin=Math.sin(angle),delta=angle-rest.angle;
  return {x:s.x+(lx*scale*Math.cos(angle)-ly*scale*Math.sin(angle)),y:s.y+(lx*scale*Math.sin(angle)+ly*scale*Math.cos(angle)),angle:delta,scale};
}
function drawEthvelleFaceMotion(targetCtx,char,bStart,bEnd){
  const face=char.autoRig?.face,mesh=char.skinMesh;
  if(!face||!mesh||!mesh.restBones?.head)return;
  const t=ethvelleFaceTime();
  const hash=String(char.id||'c').split('').reduce((a,c)=>a+c.charCodeAt(0),0)%37/37;
  const phase=((t+hash*1.7+0.8)%3.7+3.7)%3.7;
  const blink=phase<0.105 || (phase>3.48&&phase<3.58);
  const clipKey=(char.id===activeCharacter()?.id&&activeClip)?activeClip.animKey:(char.clip?.animKey||null);
  const talking=(clipKey==='talk'||clipKey==='talkBlink') || char.faceMotion?.talking===true;
  const skin= /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(face.skinColor||'')?face.skinColor:'#dba982';
  function drawFeature(name,mode){
    const f=face[name];if(!f)return;
    if(mode==='eye'&&!blink)return;
    if(mode==='mouth'&&!talking)return;
    const center=ethvelleMapFacePoint(char,f,bStart,bEnd);if(!center)return;
    const w=Math.max(1,Number(f.w||.02)*mesh.srcW*center.scale),h=Math.max(1,Number(f.h||.01)*mesh.srcH*center.scale);
    targetCtx.save();targetCtx.translate(center.x,center.y);targetCtx.rotate(center.angle);
    targetCtx.fillStyle=skin;targetCtx.beginPath();targetCtx.ellipse(0,0,w*.72,h*.9,0,0,Math.PI*2);targetCtx.fill();
    targetCtx.lineCap='round';targetCtx.lineJoin='round';
    if(mode==='eye'){
      if(blink){targetCtx.strokeStyle='#392922';targetCtx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,w*.10);targetCtx.beginPath();targetCtx.moveTo(-w*.48,h*.05);targetCtx.quadraticCurveTo(0,h*.34,w*.48,h*.05);targetCtx.stroke();}
    }else if(mode==='mouth'&&(talking)){
      const open=.10+.5*(.5+.5*Math.sin(t*16.5+hash*3));
      targetCtx.fillStyle='#3e1720';targetCtx.strokeStyle='#3e1720';targetCtx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,w*.055);
      targetCtx.beginPath();targetCtx.ellipse(0,0,w*.47,Math.max(h*.12,h*open),0,0,Math.PI*2);targetCtx.fill();targetCtx.stroke();
      if(open>.34){targetCtx.fillStyle='#e9788a';targetCtx.beginPath();targetCtx.ellipse(0,h*open*.43,w*.22,h*.10,0,0,Math.PI*2);targetCtx.fill();}
    }
    targetCtx.restore();
  }
  drawFeature('leftEye','eye');drawFeature('rightEye','eye');drawFeature('mouth','mouth');
}

// Add the facial pass after skin drawing for both the live canvas and export context.
(function attachFacePass(){
  if(typeof drawCustomCharacterOnContext!=='function')return;
  const original=drawCustomCharacterOnContext;
  drawCustomCharacterOnContext=function(targetCtx,char,boneStartFn,boneEndFn){
    original(targetCtx,char,boneStartFn,boneEndFn);
    try{drawEthvelleFaceMotion(targetCtx,char,boneStartFn||getBoneStart,boneEndFn||getBoneEnd);}catch(e){console.debug('Ethvelle face pass skipped',e);}
  };
})();

// Additional animation clips: talk drives the mouth overlay; gesture poses bend detected finger bones.
Object.assign(animPresets,{
  talk:{name:'Talk + blink',duration:2.4,keyframes:[
    {t:0,pose:{head:0,spine:0,armL:0,armR:0,gestureFist:0,gesturePoint:0,gesturePeace:0,gestureThumbsUp:0}},
    {t:.25,pose:{head:-4*D,spine:1.5*D,armL:-3*D,armR:3*D,gestureFist:0}},
    {t:.5,pose:{head:2*D,spine:-1.5*D,armL:2*D,armR:-2*D,gestureFist:0}},
    {t:.75,pose:{head:-2*D,spine:1*D,armL:-2*D,armR:2*D,gestureFist:0}},
    {t:1,pose:{head:0,spine:0,armL:0,armR:0,gestureFist:0}}
  ]},
  fist:{name:'Fist pose',duration:1,keyframes:[{t:0,pose:{gestureFist:1}},{t:1,pose:{gestureFist:1}}]},
  point:{name:'Point pose',duration:1,keyframes:[{t:0,pose:{gesturePoint:1}},{t:1,pose:{gesturePoint:1}}]},
  peace:{name:'Peace sign',duration:1,keyframes:[{t:0,pose:{gesturePeace:1}},{t:1,pose:{gesturePeace:1}}]},
  thumbsUp:{name:'Thumbs up',duration:1,keyframes:[{t:0,pose:{gestureThumbsUp:1}},{t:1,pose:{gestureThumbsUp:1}}]}
});

// Drive facial motion from the same absolute timeline time used by the editor and the exported frames.
(function trackExportFaceTime(){
  // Patch the export loop's absolute timestamp without changing any media-export behavior.
  const scripts=[...document.scripts];
  const last=scripts[scripts.length-1];
  // drawEthvelleFaceMotion reads faceAnimationTimeOverride; global rendering sets currentAbsoluteTimeSec.
  // The export loop below uses a local clipAbsoluteT, which is assigned in the hook if the optional callback exists.
  window.ethvelleSetFaceTime=t=>{faceAnimationTimeOverride=Number.isFinite(t)?t:null;};
})();
</script>

// Z-order (back to front). Within each limb the most DISTAL piece is drawn first and the more PROXIMAL
// piece after, so its joint end visually overlaps and hides the seam -- hand first, then forearm (whose
// elbow end overlaps the hand's wrist), then upper arm last (whose cuff overlaps the forearm's elbow).
const SHEET_DEFAULT_ORDER = ['tail','shoeR','shoeL','legR_lower','legL_lower','legR_upper','legL_upper',
  'handL','handL_fist','handL_point','handL_thumb','handL_palm','handL_hold','armL_lower','armL_upper',
