// SIDE VIEW (pure profile, facing right): the R-side limbs are the FAR limbs and are drawn BEHIND the body,
// the L-side limbs are the NEAR limbs and are drawn IN FRONT of it. Their silhouettes are the same, so they
// are never mirrored; see edDefaultLayout (which switches the mirror heuristic off) and edStanceOf. Within
// each limb this view's existing convention runs the other way from the front view (upper arm first, then
// forearm, then hand last), so the new hand piece is simply appended after its forearm, continuing that trend.
const SHEET_DEFAULT_ORDER_BIRD_7 = ['legR_upper','armR_upper','wingR_fly','torso','tail','head','beak_closed','beak_open','bird_eye_closed','bird_eye','bird_brow','legL_upper','armL_upper','wingL_fly'];
const SHEET_DEFAULT_ORDER_HUMANOID_FACEKIT = [...SHEET_DEFAULT_ORDER,'eye_closed_L','eye_closed_R','eye_L','eye_R','brow_L','brow_R'];
const SHEET_DEFAULT_ORDER_WINGED = ['wingL','wingR',...SHEET_DEFAULT_ORDER];
// Mythic depth order: tail fin -> middle -> waist root, then the normal far/body/near layering.
const SHEET_DEFAULT_ORDER_MYTHIC = ['tail_tip','tail_mid','tail','handL','handL_fist','handL_point','handL_thumb','handL_palm','handL_hold','armL_lower','armL_upper','torso','mouth_inner','head','head_blink','head_open','head_sad','head_angry','head_shock','head_laugh','mouth_closed','mouth_open','mouth_hold','eye_closed_L','eye_closed_R','eye_L','eye_R','brow_L','brow_R','handR','handR_fist','handR_point','handR_thumb','handR_palm','handR_hold','armR_lower','armR_upper'];
const SHEET_DEFAULT_ORDER_SIDE = ['tail','legR_upper','legR_lower','shoeR','armR_upper','armR_lower','handR','handR_fist','handR_point','handR_thumb','handR_palm','handR_hold','torso','mouth_inner','head','head_blink','head_open','head_sad','head_angry','head_shock','head_laugh','eye_closed_L','eye_closed_R','eye_L','eye_R','brow_L','brow_R','mouth_closed','mouth_open','mouth_hold',
  'legL_upper','legL_lower','shoeL','armL_upper','armL_lower','handL','handL_fist','handL_point','handL_thumb','handL_palm','handL_hold'];
// Normalize depth order for humanoids: BOTH arm/hand chains must sit in front of the torso AND the legs
// (pants). Earlier builds forced one arm behind the body, which hid that hand under the hoodie/pants
// while the other arm stayed on top. Arm pieces that are already in front are left exactly where they
// are, so a deliberate front-side layering from the editor is preserved.
// Bird tail artwork is always layered over body art.
function sheetDepthOrderForView(order, modelType, view){
  let out=Array.isArray(order)?order.slice():[];
  // v352: Mermaid and Naga / Lamia both need the head artwork behind the torso.
  // The body overlaps the lower jaw/neck join so the head does not look pasted onto
  // the front of the base body. Keep every full-expression-head swap in the same slot;
  // independently nested eyes and brows are still painted in the face pass.
  if(modelType==='lamia'||modelType==='mermaid'){
    const backHeadKeys=['head','head_blink','head_open','head_sad','head_angry','head_shock','head_laugh'];
    const headParts=out.filter(k=>backHeadKeys.includes(k));
    out=out.filter(k=>!backHeadKeys.includes(k));
    const torsoAt=out.indexOf('torso');
    out.splice(torsoAt<0?0:torsoAt,0,...headParts);
  }
  if(modelType==='bird'){
    const ti=out.indexOf('tail'),bi=out.indexOf('torso');
    if(ti>=0&&bi>=0&&ti<bi){out.splice(ti,1);out.splice(out.indexOf('torso')+1,0,'tail');}
    // v325: the closed-eye lid must sit UNDER the open eye, never on top of it.
    const bci=out.indexOf('bird_eye_closed'),boi=out.indexOf('bird_eye');
    if(bci>=0&&boi>=0&&bci>boi){out.splice(bci,1);out.splice(out.indexOf('bird_eye'),0,'bird_eye_closed');}
  }
  if(modelType==='humanoid'||isWingedHumanoidType(modelType)){
    const chainL=['handL','handL_fist','handL_point','handL_thumb','handL_palm','handL_hold','armL_lower','armL_upper'];
    const chainR=['handR','handR_fist','handR_point','handR_thumb','handR_palm','handR_hold','armR_lower','armR_upper'];
    const armKeys=[...chainL,...chainR];
    const bodyKeys=['torso','legL_upper','legL_lower','shoeL','legR_upper','legR_lower','shoeR'];
    // v324 FRONT VIEW: the viewer-left arm (L keys) is the character's RIGHT arm and stays IN FRONT of the
    // body, pants and shoes. The viewer-right arm (R keys) is the character's LEFT arm and is tucked UNDER
    // the body and pants (drawn first). Distal-first order inside each limb is preserved.
    if(!view || view==='front'){
      const under=out.filter(k=>chainR.includes(k));
      out=out.filter(k=>!chainR.includes(k));
      out.unshift(...under);
      let lastBodyF=-1;
      out.forEach((k,i)=>{ if(bodyKeys.includes(k)) lastBodyF=i; });
      if(lastBodyF>=0){
        const behindL=out.filter((k,i)=>chainL.includes(k)&&i<lastBodyF);
        if(behindL.length){
          out=out.filter((k,i)=>!(chainL.includes(k)&&i<lastBodyF));
          out.push(...behindL);
        }
      }
      return out;
    }
    let lastBody=-1;
    out.forEach((k,i)=>{ if(bodyKeys.includes(k)) lastBody=i; });
    if(lastBody>=0){
      const behind=out.filter((k,i)=>armKeys.includes(k)&&i<lastBody);
      if(behind.length){
        out=out.filter((k,i)=>!(armKeys.includes(k)&&i<lastBody));
        // keep distal-first order inside each limb, and keep the arms after body/legs/head
        out.push(...behind);
      }
    }
  }
  return out;
}
const SHEET_LABEL_SIDE = { armL_upper:'Near arm', armL_lower:'Near forearm', handL:'Near hand', armR_upper:'Far arm', armR_lower:'Far forearm', handR:'Far hand',
  legL_upper:'Near thigh', legL_lower:'Near shin', shoeL:'Near shoe', legR_upper:'Far thigh', legR_lower:'Far shin', shoeR:'Far shoe', handL_fist:'Near fist', handR_fist:'Far fist',
  handL_point:'Near point', handR_point:'Far point', handL_thumb:'Near thumbs up', handR_thumb:'Far thumbs up',
  handL_palm:'Near open palm', handR_palm:'Far open palm' };
function sheetLabel(k){
  const type = ED.modelType || 'humanoid';
  if(isMythicModelType(type)){
    const labels={tail:'Tail base / waist joint',tail_mid:'Tail middle segment',tail_tip:type==='mermaid'?'Tail fin / fluke':type==='ghost'?'Ectoplasm tip':'Tail tip'};
    return labels[k] || SHEET_LABEL[k] || k;
  }
  if(isWingedHumanoidType(type) && (k==='wingL'||k==='wingR')) return k==='wingL'?'Left back wing':'Right back wing';
  if(type === 'animal'){
    const labels={head:'Head / muzzle',torso:'Body',tail:'Tail',armL_upper:'Near front leg (upper)',armL_lower:'Near front leg (lower)',handL:'Near front paw',armR_upper:'Far front leg (upper)',armR_lower:'Far front leg (lower)',handR:'Far front paw',legL_upper:'Near hind leg (upper)',legL_lower:'Near hind leg (lower)',shoeL:'Near hind paw',legR_upper:'Far hind leg (upper)',legR_lower:'Far hind leg (lower)',shoeR:'Far hind paw',head_blink:'Head (blink)',mouth_closed:'Muzzle (closed)',mouth_open:'Muzzle (open)',head_sad:'Head (sad)',head_angry:'Head (angry)',head_shock:'Head (startled)',head_laugh:'Head (happy)',mouth_inner:'Inner mouth (behind head)',eye_L:'Open eye (left)',eye_R:'Open eye (right)',eye_closed_L:'Closed eye (left)',eye_closed_R:'Closed eye (right)',brow_L:'Eyebrow (left)',brow_R:'Eyebrow (right)',mouth_hold:'Muzzle (holding grip)',handL_fist:'Near paw (curled)',handR_fist:'Far paw (curled)',handL_point:'Near paw (raised)',handR_point:'Far paw (raised)',handL_thumb:'Near paw (extended)',handR_thumb:'Far paw (extended)',handL_palm:'Near open paw',handR_palm:'Far open paw'};
    return labels[k] || SHEET_LABEL[k] || k;
  }
  if(type === 'bird'){
    const labels={head:'Head (blank face)',torso:'Body (no wings)',tail:'Tail feathers',armL_upper:'Near default wing',armR_upper:'Far default wing',legL_upper:'Near complete leg and foot',legR_upper:'Far complete leg and foot',bird_eye:'Bird eye (open)',bird_eye_closed:'Bird eye (closed)',bird_brow:'Bird eyebrow',beak_closed:'Closed beak',beak_open:'Open beak',wingL_fly:'Near flying wing',wingR_fly:'Far flying wing',armL_lower:'Near wing (legacy lower)',handL:'Near wingtip (legacy)',armR_lower:'Far wing (legacy lower)',handR:'Far wingtip (legacy)',legL_lower:'Near lower leg (legacy)',legR_lower:'Far lower leg (legacy)',head_blink:'Head (legacy blink)',mouth_closed:'Beak (legacy closed)',mouth_open:'Beak (legacy open)',head_sad:'Head (legacy sad)',head_angry:'Head (legacy alert)',head_shock:'Head (legacy surprised)',head_laugh:'Head (legacy happy)',mouth_hold:'Beak (legacy grip)'};
    return labels[k] || SHEET_LABEL[k] || k;
  }
  if(ED.view === 'side' && SHEET_LABEL_SIDE[k]) return SHEET_LABEL_SIDE[k];
  // v324: in the FRONT view the character faces the viewer, so the piece on the viewer's LEFT is the
  // character's own RIGHT arm/hand (and vice versa). Arm and hand names are shown by the character's side.
  if(ED.view !== 'side' && ED.view !== 'back'){
    const m = /^(arm|hand)([LR])(_.*)?$/.exec(k);
    if(m){ const swapped = m[1] + (m[2]==='L'?'R':'L') + (m[3]||''); if(SHEET_LABEL[swapped]) return SHEET_LABEL[swapped]; }
  }
  return SHEET_LABEL[k];
}
const SHEET_FILL = 0.68;      // fraction of the stage height a new model fills (leaves headroom for jumps)
// Alternate textures ("expression swaps"). SHEET_ALTS[base] lists every texture that can stand in for `base`: the blink and
// the expression heads replace the head, the open mouth replaces the closed one, and the fist / pointing / thumbs-up /
// open-palm textures replace the plain hand (NOT the forearm -- the forearm is untouched by a gesture swap now that it
// is its own piece). SHEET_ALT_OF is the reverse lookup, used to skip an alt when iterating parts normally (it never
// gets its own independent slot in the draw order -- it stands in for its base).
const SHEET_ALTS = {
  head:['head_blink','head_open','head_sad','head_angry','head_shock','head_laugh'],
  mouth_closed:['mouth_open','mouth_hold'],
  beak_closed:['beak_open'],
  armL_upper:['wingL_fly'], armR_upper:['wingR_fly'],
  handL:['handL_fist','handL_point','handL_thumb','handL_palm','handL_hold'],
  handR:['handR_fist','handR_point','handR_thumb','handR_palm','handR_hold']
};
const SHEET_ALT_OF = {};
Object.keys(SHEET_ALTS).forEach(b=>SHEET_ALTS[b].forEach(a=>{ SHEET_ALT_OF[a] = b; }));
// The expression heads are complete heads (their own mouth included), so the closed/open mouth pieces are not drawn over them.
const SHEET_EMO_HEADS = ['head_sad','head_angry','head_shock','head_laugh'];
// Given a base key and a character's expression state, returns which key should actually be drawn.
// `parts` is whatever object holds the sheet's known part keys (sh.parts at runtime, ED.arts while
// editing) -- if the alt texture isn't actually part of this particular model, always falls back to
// the base so an expression flag left set on an old/basic model can never blank out a part.
function sheetActiveKey(baseKey, expr, parts){
  if(!expr || !parts) return baseKey;
  if(baseKey === 'head'){
    const emo = expr.emo ? 'head_'+expr.emo : null;
    // Keep expression heads intact; blinking is composited as an eye-only overlay for moods.
    if(expr.eyeState==='closed' && !(emo && parts[emo])) return parts.head_blink ? 'head_blink' : baseKey;
    if(expr.eyeState==='open' && !(emo && parts[emo]) && parts.head_open) return 'head_open';
    if((expr.blink || expr.ambientBlink) && !(emo && parts[emo])) return parts.head_blink ? 'head_blink' : baseKey;
    return emo && parts[emo] ? emo : baseKey;
  }
  if(baseKey === 'beak_closed' && expr.mouth === 'open' && parts.beak_open) return 'beak_open';
  if((baseKey === 'armL_upper' || baseKey === 'armR_upper') && expr.flying){ const fly=baseKey==='armL_upper'?'wingL_fly':'wingR_fly'; if(parts[fly]) return fly; }
  if(baseKey === 'mouth_closed') {
    if(expr.mouth === 'open' && parts.mouth_open) return 'mouth_open';
    if(expr.mouth === 'hold' && parts.mouth_hold) return 'mouth_hold';
    return baseKey;
  }
  const side = baseKey === 'handL' ? 'L' : baseKey === 'handR' ? 'R' : '';
  if(side){
    if((side === 'L' ? expr.fistL : expr.fistR) && parts['hand'+side+'_fist']) return 'hand'+side+'_fist';
    const g = side === 'L' ? expr.gestL : expr.gestR;
    if(g && parts['hand'+side+'_'+g]) return 'hand'+side+'_'+g;
  }
  return baseKey;
}

// Fresh per-character expression state (blink / mouth / fists). `ambientBlink` is the random idle blink; it is
// kept separate from `blink` (driven by the Talk + Blink clip) so the two can never fight or leave the eyes stuck.
function sheetExprFresh(){ return { blink:false, mouth:'closed', eyeState:'auto', fistL:false, fistR:false, flying:false, ambientBlink:false, faceKitHideMouth:false, emo:null, gestL:null, gestR:null, customExpressionId:null, customGestureLId:null, customGestureRId:null }; }

// Shared prompt rules. Every sheet gets the same joint, colour and layout rules so the image AI output stays consistent.
const sheetCanvas = ratio => "Canvas " + ratio + ". Export with a FULLY TRANSPARENT background (a real alpha channel, nothing behind the pieces -- not a solid color, not a checkerboard, not a matte). Keep edges crisp with no background-colored halo. If your tool truly cannot export transparency, use a pure solid BLACK background instead, and draw the character with NO black or near-black outlines or clothing, so the black can be removed cleanly.";
const SHEET_P_ROUNDED = "ROUNDED, BORDERLESS JOINT ENDS (very important, for smooth animation): every piece that joins another piece must END IN A SOFT, ROUNDED CAP at that joint, like a rounded pill or capsule end. Never a flat straight cut and never a sharp corner at a joint. The shoulder end of each upper arm, the elbow end of each forearm, the wrist end of each hand, the hip end of each thigh, the knee end of each shin, the ankle end of each foot and the neck stub of the head are all smooth and fully rounded, reaching a little past the joint so the piece can rotate with no gap or hard edge showing. The torso's shoulder and hip edges are rounded too, and so are sleeve cuffs and trouser hems. At EVERY edge where one piece mates with or overlaps another, omit the black/dark outer stroke completely on that hidden attachment edge; continue the exact local fill colour to the edge. Keep dark outlines ONLY on exposed external contours, never on internal joining edges. No dark seam, border, shadow or halo at a joint.";
const SHEET_P_COLOR = "COLOR CONSISTENCY (no darkening): every piece must use EXACTLY the same colors and the same brightness as its partner. Do NOT darken, shade, tint or dim any piece, including the pieces on the viewer's right. A left and right piece must look identical in color. Flat, even lighting only: no shading tones, no cast shadows, no ambient occlusion, no darker folds or edges.";
const SHEET_P_COLOR_SIDE = "COLOR CONSISTENCY (no darkening): use one set of flat colors with even brightness across the whole character. The far-side pieces (the limbs and hands on the far side of the body) must be EXACTLY the same colors and brightness as their near partners. Do NOT make the far side darker, dimmer, desaturated or shaded, and do not add depth shading to make a far limb look like it is behind the body. Flat, even lighting only: no shading tones, no cast shadows, no ambient occlusion, no darker folds or edges.";
const SHEET_P_RULES = "Rules: no text, labels, numbers, extra pieces or perspective. Same outline weight on every piece. Every piece fully visible and uncropped.";
const SHEET_P_MIRROR = "IMPORTANT -- mirroring: piece 6 must be a true MIRROR IMAGE of piece 3 (not the same drawing repeated), piece 7 a true mirror image of piece 4, piece 8 a true mirror image of piece 5, piece 11 a true mirror image of piece 9 and piece 12 a true mirror image of piece 10. If the right arm or leg is just a copy of the left one, the finished hand or foot will look anatomically backwards on that side.";
const SHEET_P_MIRROR_EXT = "IMPORTANT -- mirroring: piece 6 must be a true MIRROR IMAGE of piece 3, piece 7 of piece 4, piece 8 of piece 5, piece 11 of piece 9, piece 12 of piece 10, piece 21 of piece 20, piece 23 of piece 22, piece 25 of piece 24 and piece 27 of piece 26. Every right-side hand or foot must be a mirror image of its left-side partner, not a copy, or it will look anatomically backwards. const SHEET_P_MIRROR_BACK = "IMPORTANT -- mirroring: a back view is symmetric, so piece 6 must be a true MIRROR IMAGE of piece 3 (not the same drawing repeated), piece 7 a true mirror image of piece 4, piece 8 of piece 5, piece 11 of piece 9 and piece 12 of piece 10. If the right arm or leg is just a copy of the left one, the finished hand or foot will look anatomically backwards on that side.";
const SHEET_P_NOMIRROR = "IMPORTANT -- NO MIRRORING IN SIDE VIEW: pieces 6, 7, 8, 11 and 12 are the far-side pieces. Each far piece has the SAME silhouette as its near partner (pieces 3, 4, 5, 9 and 10). Do NOT mirror or flip them. A mirrored hand or foot would point the wrong way.";

The heads and mouths face the viewer's right in profile and are not mirrored either.";
const SHEET_P_NOSLEEVE = "BARE FOREARMS AND HANDS (no sleeve): every forearm piece and every hand piece -- including every fist, pointing, thumbs-up and open-palm version of the hand -- is COMPLETELY BARE skin, with NO sleeve, NO cuff and NO fabric of any kind, even if the character's upper arm and sleeve are long. Sleeve fabric belongs ONLY on the upper-arm piece, and it ends cleanly at the elbow -- nothing carries over onto the forearm or hand pieces.";
const SHEET_P_HANDFRAME = "HAND-GESTURE FRAMING: every fist, pointing, thumbs-up and open-palm piece REPLACES ONLY the plain hand piece -- same wrist position, same size, same framing as the open hand it is based on. The forearm is a separate piece and is never redrawn for a gesture; only the hand and fingers change shape between versions.";
const SHEET_P_MOUTH_EDGE = "MOUTH-PIECE EDGE BLENDING (important): pieces 14 and 15 are only mouth/lower-face patches that sit directly on top of the main head. Their OUTER silhouette must have NO black, dark-brown, or dark outline at all; use the exact same flat skin colour right to the outside edge as the main face so the patch disappears into the face when overlaid. Keep dark lines ONLY for the actual smile/lips/inside of the open mouth. Do not draw a rectangular border around either mouth piece, and do not add a shadow, halo, or background fringe.";

// Turned view: the head/face is drawn straight-on, fully facing the viewer (exactly like the front view's
// head), but the torso and every limb are drawn in a PURE SIDE PROFILE -- not a subtle 3/4 turn like the
// angled view, but the same full profile body as the side view. This is the "face front, body side" look
// (think of a simple explainer-video mascot standing in profile with its face turned back to the camera).
// Because the body is genuinely side anatomy (one near limb, one far limb, no mirroring), this reuses the
// SIDE view's layout, rig and NEAR/FAR part pairing -- only this prompt text and the view picker are new.
// See buildFromSheet(), which maps sheetView 'turned' onto the 'side' layout path the same way 'angled'
// already maps onto 'front'.

    'STRAIGHT LEG SHAPES AND COLOUR: all four upper-leg pieces and all four lower-leg pieces must be straight, relaxed, unbent segments in a natural vertical hanging pose. Do NOT bend, curve, fold, crouch, angle or flex the first two leg pieces; make them as straight as the other straight leg examples. UPPER-LEG COLOUR RULE: all four upper-leg pieces (4, 5, 10 and 11) are filled with ONE single flat colour only - the main body coat colour, identical to the colour of the torso (for example pure orange on a fox) - from the top edge to the bottom edge. There is NO second colour anywhere on an upper-leg piece: no dark brown or black lower tip, no sock or stocking, no zigzag or jagged colour boundary, no white or cream patch, no gradient, no shading and no markings, even when the reference leg changes colour part-way down. The colour change from the body colour to the darker leg or foot colour happens only on the lower-leg pieces and paws, never on an upper-leg piece. Placed side by side, the four upper-leg pieces must look like four plain single-colour capsules (the thin dark outline around each piece is still drawn; it is not a second fill colour). All lower-leg pieces may use only the two flat coat colours actually visible on that matching leg in the reference; do not invent a third colour, gradient or shadow. Keep those colour rules consistent across both front and hind leg pairs. Every lower-leg piece ends cleanly at the ankle and has NO foot, paw, toes or claws attached. The four paw-only pieces remain separate and contain the paw/foot shape only, not extra shin length.',
    'EQUAL LEG THICKNESS RULE (front legs and back legs are the same): the two HIND (back) legs must NOT be thicker, wider, rounder or bulkier than the two FRONT legs. Draw the near-side front upper leg first, then draw the far-side front upper leg and BOTH hind upper legs (pieces 4, 5, 10 and 11) as exact copies of it: the same width, the same length and the same slim capsule shape. NO big thigh, NO haunch bulge, NO hock bulge, NO wider top on the hind legs. Do the same for the lower legs: the four lower-leg pieces (6, 8, 12 and 13) have the same width and the same length as each other, and the four paw pieces (7, 9, 14 and 15) are the same size. A hind leg that is even slightly thicker than a front leg is a mistake - redraw it at the front-leg thickness. This rule overrides the real anatomy of the reference animal.',
